    #!/usr/bin/env python3
    """Derive the district-heat flexibility programme reports from one season 
extract.
    
    Usage: python3 derive_report.py DATA_DIR OUT_DIR
    """
    
    import configparser
    import csv
    import json
    import os
    import sys
    import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
    from collections import defaultdict
    from datetime import datetime
    
    LABELS = (
        "SAFETY_HOLD",
        "COOLDOWN",
        "CONTINUITY_WAIVED",
        "CERTIFICATE_PENDING",
        "CLEAR",
    )
    
    
    def half_up(num, den):
        return (2 * num + den) // (2 * den)
    
    
    def half_even(num, den):
        q, r = divmod(num, den)
        twice = 2 * r
        if twice > den or (twice == den and q % 2):
            q += 1
        return q
    
    
    def read_delimited(path, delimiter):
        with open(path, encoding="utf-8", newline="") as fh:
            return list(csv.DictReader(fh, delimiter=delimiter))
    
    
    def read_commented_delimited(path, delimiter):
        with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            lines = [
                line
                for line in fh.read().splitlines()
                if line.strip() and not line.startswith("#")
            ]
        return list(csv.DictReader(lines, delimiter=delimiter))
    
    
    def read_fixed_width(path):
        with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            return [
                line
                for line in fh.read().splitlines()
                if line.strip() and not line.startswith("#")
            ]
    
    
    class Extract:
        def __init__(self, data_dir):
            self.dir = data_dir
            self.dq = {}
            self.load_config()
            self.load_events()
            self.load_sites()
            self.load_offers()
            self.load_profiles()
            self.load_targets()
            self.load_feeder_limits()
            self.load_state_and_waivers()
            self.load_floors()
            self.load_rate_cards()
            self.load_weights()
            self.load_pools()
            self.load_allowances()
            self.load_manifest()
    
        def path(self, name):
            return os.path.join(self.dir, name)
    
        # ---------------------------------------------------------------- 
config
        def load_config(self):
            cfg = configparser.ConfigParser()
            with open(self.path("program.ini"), encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                cfg.read_file(fh)
            self.wh_per_tick = cfg.getint("units", "wh_per_tick")
            self.millicents_per_cent = cfg.getint("units", 
"millicents_per_cent")
            self.region_of_code = {k: v for k, v in cfg["region_codes"].items()}
            self.segment_of_code = {k: v for k, v in 
cfg["segment_codes"].items()}
            self.region_names = sorted(set(self.region_of_code.values()))
            self.segment_names = sorted(set(self.segment_of_code.values()))
            adders = cfg["segment_rate_adders"] if 
cfg.has_section("segment_rate_adders") else {}
            self.segment_adder = {
                name: int(adders.get(name.lower(), 0)) for name in 
self.segment_names
            }
            self.claim_cents_per_weight = cfg.getint("grants", 
"claim_cents_per_weight")
            self.minimum_grant_cents = cfg.getint("grants", 
"minimum_grant_cents")
    
        def region_name(self, code):
            return self.region_of_code.get(code.strip().lower())
    
        def segment_name(self, code):
            return self.segment_of_code.get(code.strip().lower())
    
        def ticks(self, wh):
            return half_up(wh, self.wh_per_tick)
    
        # ---------------------------------------------------------------- 
events
        def load_events(self):
            with open(self.path("events.json"), encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                raw = json.load(fh)
            self.dq_rows_events = len(raw)
            self.events = {}
            for entry in raw:
                self.events[entry["event_id"]] = {
                    "event_id": entry["event_id"],
                    "interval_count": int(entry["interval_count"]),
                    "cost_weight": int(entry["objective"]["cost_weight"]),
                    "recovery_weight": 
int(entry["objective"]["recovery_weight"]),
                    "uplift": int(entry["uplift_millicents_per_kwh"]),
                    "start": datetime.fromisoformat(entry["dispatch_start"]),
                }
            self.event_ids = sorted(self.events)
            # Season order: ascending start instant; events sharing an instant 
are
            # reached in descending event_id (programme conventions).
            by_id_desc = sorted(self.event_ids, reverse=True)
            self.season_order = sorted(by_id_desc, key=lambda eid: 
self.events[eid]["start"])
    
        # ----------------------------------------------------------------- 
sites
        def load_sites(self):
            rows = read_delimited(self.path("sites.csv"), ",")
            self.dq_rows_sites = len(rows)
            self.sites = {}
            self.unknown_region_site_rows = 0
            self.site_ids = []
            seen_site = set()
            for row in rows:
                site_id = row["site_id"]
                if site_id not in seen_site:
                    seen_site.add(site_id)
                    self.site_ids.append(site_id)
                region = self.region_name(row["region_code"])
                if region is None:
                    self.unknown_region_site_rows += 1
                    continue
                self.sites[(row["event_id"], site_id)] = {
                    "event_id": row["event_id"],
                    "site_id": site_id,
                    "region": region,
                    "segment": self.segment_name(row["segment_code"]),
                    "beneficiary_id": row["beneficiary_id"],
                    "critical": row["critical"] == "Y",
                }
    
        # ---------------------------------------------------------------- 
offers
        def load_offers(self):
            rows = read_delimited(self.path("offers.csv"), ",")
            self.dq_rows_offers = len(rows)
            self.offers = {}
            self.orphan_offer_rows = 0
            for row in rows:
                key = (row["event_id"], row["site_id"])
                if key not in self.sites:
                    self.orphan_offer_rows += 1
                    continue
                n = self.events[row["event_id"]]["interval_count"]
                self.offers[row["offer_id"]] = {
                    "offer_id": row["offer_id"],
                    "event_id": row["event_id"],
                    "site_id": row["site_id"],
                    "bid_cost_cents": int(row["bid_cost_cents"]),
                    "relief": [0] * n,
                    "recovery": [0] * n,
                    "relief_total": 0,
                    "recovery_total": 0,
                }
            self.offer_event_count = {}
            for row in rows:
                self.offer_event_count[row["offer_id"]] = row["event_id"]
    
        # -------------------------------------------------------------- 
profiles
        def load_profiles(self):
            rows = read_delimited(self.path("profiles.tsv"), "\t")
            self.dq_rows_profiles = len(rows)
            self.orphan_profile_rows = 0
            self.out_of_range_profile_rows = 0
            self.duplicate_profile_rows = 0
            kept = set()
            for row in rows:
                offer_id = row["offer_id"]
                event_id = self.offer_event_count.get(offer_id)
                if event_id is None:
                    self.orphan_profile_rows += 1
                    continue
                interval = int(row["interval"])
                if interval < 1 or interval > 
self.events[event_id]["interval_count"]:
                    self.out_of_range_profile_rows += 1
                    continue
                if (offer_id, interval) in kept:
                    self.duplicate_profile_rows += 1
                    continue
                kept.add((offer_id, interval))
                offer = self.offers.get(offer_id)
                if offer is None:
                    continue
                mult = int(row["register_multiplier"])
                relief = self.ticks(int(row["relief_wh"]) * mult)
                recovery = self.ticks(int(row["recovery_wh"]) * mult)
                offer["relief"][interval - 1] = relief
                offer["recovery"][interval - 1] = recovery
                offer["relief_total"] += relief
                offer["recovery_total"] += recovery
    
        # --------------------------------------------------------------- 
targets
        def load_targets(self):
            rows = read_delimited(self.path("targets.tsv"), "\t")
            self.dq_rows_targets = len(rows)
            self.targets = {}
            for row in rows:
                key = (row["event_id"], self.region_name(row["region_code"]))
                n = self.events[row["event_id"]]["interval_count"]
                slot = self.targets.setdefault(key, ([0] * n, [0] * n))
                interval = int(row["interval"])
                slot[0][interval - 1] = self.ticks(int(row["target_wh"]))
                slot[1][interval - 1] = int(row["shortfall_weight"])
    
        def target_of(self, event_id, region):
            n = self.events[event_id]["interval_count"]
            return self.targets.get((event_id, region), ([0] * n, [0] * n))
    
        # ---------------------------------------------------------- feeder 
limits
        def load_feeder_limits(self):
            lines = read_fixed_width(self.path("feeder_limits.txt"))
            self.dq_rows_feeder = len(lines)
            self.ceilings = {}
            for line in lines:
                event_id = line[0:10].strip()
                region = self.region_name(line[10:14])
                interval = int(line[14:17])
                relief = int(line[17:27])
                recovery = int(line[27:37])
                n = self.events[event_id]["interval_count"]
                slot = self.ceilings.setdefault((event_id, region), ([0] * n, 
[0] * n))
                slot[0][interval - 1] = self.ticks(relief)
                slot[1][interval - 1] = self.ticks(recovery)
    
        def ceiling_of(self, event_id, region):
            n = self.events[event_id]["interval_count"]
            return self.ceilings.get((event_id, region), ([0] * n, [0] * n))
    
        # ------------------------------------------------- state and waivers
        def load_state_and_waivers(self):
            with open(self.path("site_state.jsonl"), encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                lines = [line for line in fh.read().splitlines() if 
line.strip()]
            self.dq_rows_state = len(lines)
            self.state = {}
            for line in lines:
                entry = json.loads(line)
                key = (entry["event_id"], entry["site_id"])
                if key not in self.sites:
                    continue
                self.state[key] = entry
    
            rows = read_delimited(self.path("continuity_waivers.csv"), ",")
            self.dq_rows_waivers = len(rows)
            self.waiver_signed_missing = 0
            self.waivers = {}
            for row in rows:
                if row["waiver_signed"].strip() == "":
                    self.waiver_signed_missing += 1
                key = (row["event_id"], row["site_id"])
                if key not in self.sites:
                    continue
                self.waivers[key] = row
    
        # ---------------------------------------------------------------- 
floors
        def load_floors(self):
            with open(self.path("segment_floors.json"), encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                entries = json.load(fh)["floors"]
            self.dq_rows_floors = len(entries)
            self.floors = {}
            for entry in entries:
                key = (
                    entry["event_id"],
                    self.region_name(entry["region_code"]),
                    self.segment_name(entry["segment_code"]),
                )
                self.floors[key] = (
                    self.ticks(int(entry["floor_wh"])),
                    int(entry["priority_weight"]),
                )
    
        def floor_of(self, event_id, region, segment):
            return self.floors.get((event_id, region, segment), (0, 0))
    
        # ------------------------------------------------------------ rate 
cards
        def load_rate_cards(self):
            root = ET.parse(self.path("rate_cards.xml")).getroot()
            cards = root.findall("card")
            self.dq_rows_cards = len(cards)
            self.cards = {}
            for card in cards:
                key = (card.get("event"), card.get("site"))
                if key not in self.sites:
                    continue
                override = card.find("override")
                base = card.find("base")
                rate = int((override if override is not None else base).text)
                self.cards[key] = (rate, int(card.find("cap").text))
    
        # --------------------------------------------------------------- 
weights
        def load_weights(self):
            rows = read_delimited(self.path("recipient_weights.tsv"), "\t")
            self.dq_rows_weights = len(rows)
            self.grant_weights = {}
            for row in rows:
                key = (row["event_id"], row["site_id"])
                if key not in self.sites:
                    continue
                self.grant_weights[key] = int(row["grant_weight"])
    
        # ----------------------------------------------------------------- 
pools
        def load_pools(self):
            lines = read_fixed_width(self.path("pools.txt"))
            self.dq_rows_pools = len(lines)
            self.pools = {}
            for line in lines:
                event_id = line[0:10].strip()
                region = self.region_name(line[10:14])
                self.pools[(event_id, region)] = int(line[14:26])
    
        # ------------------------------------------------------------ 
allowances
        def load_allowances(self):
            rows = read_commented_delimited(self.path("site_allowances.tsv"), 
"\t")
            self.dq_rows_allowances = len(rows)
            own = {}
            draws = {}
            for row in rows:
                site_id = row["site_id"]
                own[site_id] = int(row["allowance_ticks"])
                draws[site_id] = (row.get("draws_from") or "").strip()
            self.allowance_root = {}
            for site_id in own:
                seen = []
                current = site_id
                while draws.get(current):
                    seen.append(current)
                    current = draws[current]
                for node in seen:
                    self.allowance_root[node] = current
                self.allowance_root[site_id] = current
            self.allowance_ticks = own
    
        # -------------------------------------------------------------- 
manifest
        def load_manifest(self):
            rows = read_commented_delimited(self.path("export_manifest.tsv"), 
"\t")
            self.dq_rows_manifest = len(rows)
    
    
    def compliance_label(extract, key, site):
        state = extract.state.get(key)
        waiver = extract.waivers.get(key)
        if state is not None and state["safety_hold"]:
            return "SAFETY_HOLD"
        if state is not None and state["cooldown"]:
            return "COOLDOWN"
        pending = waiver is not None and waiver["routine_certificate_pending"] 
== "Y"
        if pending and waiver is not None:
            valid = (
                waiver["waiver_status"] == "VALID"
                and waiver["waiver_signed"] == "Y"
                and site["critical"]
                and site["segment"] == "public"
            )
            if valid:
                return "CONTINUITY_WAIVED"
        if pending:
            return "CERTIFICATE_PENDING"
        return "CLEAR"
    
    
    def choose_portfolio(groups, targets, weights, ceilings, floor_list, 
objective, n):
        """Enumerate the feasible portfolios and return the best one.
    
        groups is a list of lists of candidate offers, one list per site.
        Returns (selected_offers, tied_count).
        """
        relief_ceiling, recovery_ceiling = ceilings
        target_ticks, shortfall_weight = targets
        cost_weight, recovery_weight = objective
        seg_count = len(floor_list)
        relief = [0] * n
        recovery = [0] * n
        seg_relief = [0] * seg_count
        chosen = []
        best = [None, None, 0]  # key, selected ids tuple, count
    
        def evaluate(bid):
            c1 = 0
            c4 = 0
            for t in range(n):
                diff = target_ticks[t] - relief[t]
                if diff > 0:
                    c1 += shortfall_weight[t] * diff
                elif diff < 0:
                    c4 -= diff
            c2 = 0
            for s in range(seg_count):
                floor_ticks, priority = floor_list[s]
                deficit = floor_ticks - seg_relief[s]
                if deficit > 0:
                    c2 += priority * deficit
            c3 = cost_weight * bid + recovery_weight * sum(recovery)
            key = (c1, c2, c3, c4, len(chosen))
            if best[0] is None or key < best[0]:
                best[0] = key
                best[1] = tuple(sorted(chosen))
                best[2] = 1
            elif key == best[0]:
                best[2] += 1
                ids = tuple(sorted(chosen))
                if ids > best[1]:
                    best[1] = ids
    
        def recurse(index, bid):
            if index == len(groups):
                evaluate(bid)
                return
            recurse(index + 1, bid)
            for offer, seg_index in groups[index]:
                offer_relief = offer["relief"]
                offer_recovery = offer["recovery"]
                feasible = True
                for t in range(n):
                    relief[t] += offer_relief[t]
                    recovery[t] += offer_recovery[t]
                    if relief[t] > relief_ceiling[t] or recovery[t] > 
recovery_ceiling[t]:
                        feasible = False
                if feasible:
                    seg_relief[seg_index] += offer["relief_total"]
                    chosen.append(offer["offer_id"])
                    recurse(index + 1, bid + offer["bid_cost_cents"])
                    chosen.pop()
                    seg_relief[seg_index] -= offer["relief_total"]
                for t in range(n):
                    relief[t] -= offer_relief[t]
                    recovery[t] -= offer_recovery[t]
    
        recurse(0, 0)
        return best[1], best[2]
    
    
    def largest_remainder(funded, weights):
        """Share funded cents in proportion to weights by largest remainder."""
        total = sum(weights.values())
        shares = {}
        remainders = {}
        for bene, weight in weights.items():
            product = funded * weight
            shares[bene] = product // total
            remainders[bene] = product % total
        leftover = funded - sum(shares.values())
        order = sorted(weights, reverse=True)
        order.sort(key=lambda bene: -remainders[bene])
        for bene in order[:leftover]:
            shares[bene] += 1
        counts = defaultdict(int)
        for bene, remainder in remainders.items():
            if remainder:
                counts[remainder] += 1
        tie = any(count >= 2 for count in counts.values())
        return shares, leftover, tie
    
    
    def apportion(funded, weights, claim_per_weight, minimum):
        """Return (shares, allocated, unallocated, residual, residual_tie)."""
        positive = {bene: w for bene, w in weights.items() if w > 0}
        if not weights or not positive:
            return {}, 0, funded, 0, False
        claims = {bene: w * claim_per_weight for bene, w in positive.items()}
        if sum(claims.values()) <= funded:
            allocated = sum(claims.values())
            return claims, allocated, funded - allocated, 0, False
        shares, residual, tie = largest_remainder(funded, positive)
        below = [bene for bene, cents in shares.items() if cents < minimum]
        if len(below) == len(positive):
            return {bene: 0 for bene in positive}, 0, funded, 0, False
        if below:
            keep = {bene: positive[bene] for bene in positive if bene not in 
below}
            shares, residual, tie = largest_remainder(funded, keep)
            for bene in below:
                shares[bene] = 0
        allocated = sum(shares.values())
        return shares, allocated, funded - allocated, residual, tie
    
    
    def run(data_dir, out_dir):
        ex = Extract(data_dir)
        n_den = 1000 * ex.millicents_per_cent
    
        site_rows_by_er = defaultdict(list)
        for key in sorted(ex.sites):
            site = ex.sites[key]
            site_rows_by_er[(site["event_id"], site["region"])].append(key)
    
        labels = {}
        for key in sorted(ex.sites):
            labels[key] = compliance_label(ex, key, ex.sites[key])
    
        # Effective rates and cap binding per site row.
        rates = {}
        cap_binding = {}
        for key, site in ex.sites.items():
            card = ex.cards.get(key)
            if card is None:
                continue
            rate, cap = card
            rate += ex.segment_adder.get(site["segment"], 0)
            rate += ex.events[site["event_id"]]["uplift"]
            rates[key] = min(rate, cap)
            cap_binding[key] = rate > cap
    
        offers_by_event_site = defaultdict(list)
        for offer_id in sorted(ex.offers):
            offer = ex.offers[offer_id]
            offers_by_event_site[(offer["event_id"], 
offer["site_id"])].append(offer)
    
        seg_index_of = {name: i for i, name in enumerate(ex.segment_names)}
    
        ineligible_offer_count = 0
        allowance_blocked_offer_count = 0
        empty_candidate_groups = 0
        consumed = defaultdict(int)
        carried = defaultdict(int)
        results = {}
        tie_decisions = []
        cap_binding_count = 0
        residual_cents_total = 0
        residual_tie_pool_count = 0
        unallocated_pool_count = 0
    
        for event_id in ex.season_order:
            event = ex.events[event_id]
            n = event["interval_count"]
            candidates = defaultdict(dict)  # region -> site_id -> list of 
offers
            for region in ex.region_names:
                candidates[region] = defaultdict(list)
            for key in site_rows_by_er_keys(site_rows_by_er, event_id, 
ex.region_names):
                pass
            for region in ex.region_names:
                for key in site_rows_by_er[(event_id, region)]:
                    site = ex.sites[key]
                    state = ex.state.get(key)
                    can_dispatch = state is not None and 
state["dispatch_authorized"]
                    root = ex.allowance_root.get(site["site_id"], 
site["site_id"])
                    remaining = ex.allowance_ticks.get(root, 0) - consumed[root]
                    for offer in offers_by_event_site[key]:
                        if not can_dispatch:
                            ineligible_offer_count += 1
                            continue
                        if offer["relief_total"] > remaining:
                            allowance_blocked_offer_count += 1
                            continue
                        candidates[region][site["site_id"]].append(offer)
    
            for region in ex.region_names:
                groups = []
                candidate_count = 0
                for site_id in sorted(candidates[region]):
                    offers = candidates[region][site_id]
                    if not offers:
                        continue
                    segment = ex.sites[(event_id, site_id)]["segment"]
                    groups.append([(o, seg_index_of[segment]) for o in offers])
                    candidate_count += len(offers)
                if candidate_count == 0:
                    empty_candidate_groups += 1
    
                targets = ex.target_of(event_id, region)
                ceilings = ex.ceiling_of(event_id, region)
                floor_list = [ex.floor_of(event_id, region, s) for s in 
ex.segment_names]
                selected_ids, tied_count = choose_portfolio(
                    groups,
                    targets,
                    ex.target_of(event_id, region)[1],
                    ceilings,
                    floor_list,
                    (event["cost_weight"], event["recovery_weight"]),
                    n,
                )
                selected = [ex.offers[oid] for oid in sorted(selected_ids)]
                if tied_count >= 2:
                    tie_decisions.append(
                        {
                            "event_id": event_id,
                            "region": region,
                            "tied_portfolio_count": tied_count,
                            "selected_offer_ids": sorted(selected_ids),
                        }
                    )
    
                relief = [0] * n
                recovery = [0] * n
                bid_cost = 0
                credit = 0
                offer_credits = {}
                for offer in selected:
                    key = (offer["event_id"], offer["site_id"])
                    bid_cost += offer["bid_cost_cents"]
                    rate = rates.get(key, 0)
                    amount = 0
                    for t in range(n):
                        relief[t] += offer["relief"][t]
                        recovery[t] += offer["recovery"][t]
                        credited = offer["relief"][t] - offer["recovery"][t]
                        if credited > 0:
                            amount += half_even(credited * ex.wh_per_tick * 
rate, n_den)
                    offer_credits[offer["offer_id"]] = amount
                    credit += amount
                    if cap_binding.get(key):
                        cap_binding_count += 1
                    consumed[ex.allowance_root.get(offer["site_id"], 
offer["site_id"])] += (
                        offer["relief_total"]
                    )
    
                pool = ex.pools.get((event_id, region), 0)
                funded = max(0, pool + carried[region] - credit)
                weights = defaultdict(int)
                recipient_count = 0
                for key in site_rows_by_er[(event_id, region)]:
                    if key not in ex.grant_weights:
                        continue
                    recipient_count += 1
                    weight = ex.grant_weights[key]
                    if labels[key] == "CONTINUITY_WAIVED":
                        weight *= 2
                    weights[ex.sites[key]["beneficiary_id"]] += weight
                shares, allocated, unallocated, residual, tie = apportion(
                    funded, dict(weights), ex.claim_cents_per_weight, 
ex.minimum_grant_cents
                )
                if recipient_count == 0 or sum(weights.values()) == 0:
                    unallocated_pool_count += 1
                residual_cents_total += residual
                if tie:
                    residual_tie_pool_count += 1
                carried[region] = unallocated
    
                counts = {label: 0 for label in LABELS}
                for key in site_rows_by_er[(event_id, region)]:
                    counts[labels[key]] += 1
    
                results[(event_id, region)] = {
                    "candidate_offer_count": candidate_count,
                    "selected": selected,
                    "selected_ids": sorted(selected_ids),
                    "relief": relief,
                    "recovery": recovery,
                    "targets": targets[0],
                    "bid_cost_cents": bid_cost,
                    "credit_cents": credit,
                    "offer_credits": offer_credits,
                    "pool": pool,
                    "funded": funded,
                    "allocated": allocated,
                    "unallocated": unallocated,
                    "grants": shares,
                    "compliance_counts": counts,
                }
    
        write_outputs(
            ex,
            out_dir,
            results,
            labels,
            tie_decisions,
            {
                "cap_binding_count": cap_binding_count,
                "residual_cents": residual_cents_total,
                "residual_tie_pool_count": residual_tie_pool_count,
            },
            {
                "orphan_offer_rows": ex.orphan_offer_rows,
                "orphan_profile_rows": ex.orphan_profile_rows,
                "out_of_range_profile_rows": ex.out_of_range_profile_rows,
                "duplicate_profile_rows": ex.duplicate_profile_rows,
                "unknown_region_site_rows": ex.unknown_region_site_rows,
                "waiver_signed_missing": ex.waiver_signed_missing,
                "ineligible_offer_count": ineligible_offer_count,
                "allowance_blocked_offer_count": allowance_blocked_offer_count,
                "empty_candidate_groups": empty_candidate_groups,
                "unallocated_pool_count": unallocated_pool_count,
            },
        )
    
    
    def site_rows_by_er_keys(mapping, event_id, regions):
        return []
    
    
    def dump(path, obj):
        with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            json.dump(obj, fh, sort_keys=True, indent=1)
            fh.write("\n")
    
    
    def write_outputs(ex, out_dir, results, labels, tie_decisions, audit, 
quality):
        os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)
    
        region_docs = {}
        for region in ex.region_names:
            events = []
            totals = {
                key: 0
                for key in (
                    "event_count",
                    "candidate_offer_count",
                    "selected_offer_count",
                    "relief_ticks",
                    "recovery_ticks",
                    "shortfall_ticks",
                    "overdelivery_ticks",
                    "bid_cost_cents",
                    "credit_cents",
                    "grant_pool_cents",
                    "grant_funded_cents",
                    "grant_allocated_cents",
                    "grant_unallocated_cents",
                )
            }
            for event_id in ex.event_ids:
                res = results[(event_id, region)]
                n = ex.events[event_id]["interval_count"]
                intervals = []
                for t in range(n):
                    relief = res["relief"][t]
                    target = res["targets"][t]
                    intervals.append(
                        {
                            "interval": t + 1,
                            "target_ticks": target,
                            "relief_ticks": relief,
                            "recovery_ticks": res["recovery"][t],
                            "shortfall_ticks": max(0, target - relief),
                            "overdelivery_ticks": max(0, relief - target),
                        }
                    )
                entry = {
                    "event_id": event_id,
                    "candidate_offer_count": res["candidate_offer_count"],
                    "selected_offer_ids": res["selected_ids"],
                    "intervals": intervals,
                    "bid_cost_cents": res["bid_cost_cents"],
                    "credit_cents": res["credit_cents"],
                    "grant_pool_cents": res["pool"],
                    "grant_funded_cents": res["funded"],
                    "grant_allocated_cents": res["allocated"],
                    "grant_unallocated_cents": res["unallocated"],
                    "grants": [
                        {"beneficiary_id": bene, "cents": res["grants"][bene]}
                        for bene in sorted(res["grants"])
                    ],
                    "compliance_counts": res["compliance_counts"],
                }
                events.append(entry)
                totals["event_count"] += 1
                totals["candidate_offer_count"] += res["candidate_offer_count"]
                totals["selected_offer_count"] += len(res["selected_ids"])
                totals["relief_ticks"] += sum(res["relief"])
                totals["recovery_ticks"] += sum(res["recovery"])
                totals["shortfall_ticks"] += sum(i["shortfall_ticks"] for i in 
intervals)
                totals["overdelivery_ticks"] += sum(i["overdelivery_ticks"] for 
i in intervals)
                totals["bid_cost_cents"] += res["bid_cost_cents"]
                totals["credit_cents"] += res["credit_cents"]
                totals["grant_pool_cents"] += res["pool"]
                totals["grant_funded_cents"] += res["funded"]
                totals["grant_allocated_cents"] += res["allocated"]
                totals["grant_unallocated_cents"] += res["unallocated"]
            region_docs[region] = {"region": region, "events": events, "totals":
totals}
            dump(os.path.join(out_dir, "region_%s.json" % region), 
region_docs[region])
    
        segment_docs = {}
        for segment in ex.segment_names:
            entries = []
            totals = {
                key: 0
                for key in (
                    "selected_offer_count",
                    "relief_ticks",
                    "recovery_ticks",
                    "bid_cost_cents",
                    "credit_cents",
                    "floor_ticks",
                )
            }
            for event_id in ex.event_ids:
                for region in ex.region_names:
                    res = results[(event_id, region)]
                    offer_ids = []
                    relief = 0
                    recovery = 0
                    bid_cost = 0
                    credit = 0
                    for offer in res["selected"]:
                        site = ex.sites[(offer["event_id"], offer["site_id"])]
                        if site["segment"] != segment:
                            continue
                        offer_ids.append(offer["offer_id"])
                        relief += offer["relief_total"]
                        recovery += offer["recovery_total"]
                        bid_cost += offer["bid_cost_cents"]
                        credit += res["offer_credits"][offer["offer_id"]]
                    floor_ticks = ex.floor_of(event_id, region, segment)[0]
                    if floor_ticks == 0:
                        attainment = 10000
                    else:
                        attainment = min(10000, half_up(10000 * relief, 
floor_ticks))
                    entries.append(
                        {
                            "event_id": event_id,
                            "region": region,
                            "selected_offer_ids": sorted(offer_ids),
                            "relief_ticks": relief,
                            "recovery_ticks": recovery,
                            "bid_cost_cents": bid_cost,
                            "credit_cents": credit,
                            "floor_ticks": floor_ticks,
                            "floor_attainment_bp": attainment,
                        }
                    )
                    totals["selected_offer_count"] += len(offer_ids)
                    totals["relief_ticks"] += relief
                    totals["recovery_ticks"] += recovery
                    totals["bid_cost_cents"] += bid_cost
                    totals["credit_cents"] += credit
                    totals["floor_ticks"] += floor_ticks
            segment_docs[segment] = {
                "segment": segment,
                "event_regions": entries,
                "totals": totals,
            }
            dump(os.path.join(out_dir, "segment_%s.json" % segment), 
segment_docs[segment])
    
        summary = {
            "event_count": len(ex.event_ids),
            "event_region_count": len(ex.event_ids) * len(ex.region_names),
            "site_count": len(ex.sites),
            "candidate_offer_count": 0,
            "selected_offer_count": 0,
            "relief_ticks": 0,
            "recovery_ticks": 0,
            "shortfall_ticks": 0,
            "overdelivery_ticks": 0,
            "bid_cost_cents": 0,
            "credit_cents": 0,
            "grant_pool_cents": 0,
            "grant_funded_cents": 0,
            "grant_allocated_cents": 0,
            "grant_unallocated_cents": 0,
        }
        for region, doc in region_docs.items():
            totals = doc["totals"]
            for key in summary:
                if key in totals and key not in ("event_count",):
                    summary[key] += totals[key]
        summary["regions"] = {
            region: {
                "selected_offer_count": doc["totals"]["selected_offer_count"],
                "relief_ticks": doc["totals"]["relief_ticks"],
                "bid_cost_cents": doc["totals"]["bid_cost_cents"],
                "credit_cents": doc["totals"]["credit_cents"],
                "grant_allocated_cents": doc["totals"]["grant_allocated_cents"],
            }
            for region, doc in region_docs.items()
        }
        summary["segments"] = {
            segment: {
                "selected_offer_count": doc["totals"]["selected_offer_count"],
                "relief_ticks": doc["totals"]["relief_ticks"],
                "bid_cost_cents": doc["totals"]["bid_cost_cents"],
                "credit_cents": doc["totals"]["credit_cents"],
            }
            for segment, doc in segment_docs.items()
        }
        dump(os.path.join(out_dir, "summary.json"), summary)
    
        counts = {label: 0 for label in LABELS}
        for label in labels.values():
            counts[label] += 1
        policy = {
            "compliance_counts": counts,
            "site_labels": [
                {"event_id": key[0], "site_id": key[1], "label": labels[key]}
                for key in sorted(labels)
            ],
            "tie_decisions": tie_decisions,
            "cap_binding_count": audit["cap_binding_count"],
            "residual_cents": audit["residual_cents"],
            "residual_tie_pool_count": audit["residual_tie_pool_count"],
        }
        dump(os.path.join(out_dir, "policy_audit.json"), policy)
    
        quality = dict(quality)
        quality["rows"] = {
            "events.json": ex.dq_rows_events,
            "sites.csv": ex.dq_rows_sites,
            "offers.csv": ex.dq_rows_offers,
            "profiles.tsv": ex.dq_rows_profiles,
            "targets.tsv": ex.dq_rows_targets,
            "continuity_waivers.csv": ex.dq_rows_waivers,
            "recipient_weights.tsv": ex.dq_rows_weights,
            "site_allowances.tsv": ex.dq_rows_allowances,
            "feeder_limits.txt": ex.dq_rows_feeder,
            "pools.txt": ex.dq_rows_pools,
            "site_state.jsonl": ex.dq_rows_state,
            "segment_floors.json": ex.dq_rows_floors,
            "rate_cards.xml": ex.dq_rows_cards,
            "export_manifest.tsv": ex.dq_rows_manifest,
        }
        quality["zero_floor_count"] = sum(
            1
            for event_id in ex.event_ids
            for region in ex.region_names
            for segment in ex.segment_names
            if ex.floor_of(event_id, region, segment)[0] == 0
        )
        dump(os.path.join(out_dir, "data_quality.json"), quality)
    
    
    def main(argv):
        if len(argv) != 3:
            sys.stderr.write("usage: derive_report.py DATA_DIR OUT_DIR\n")
            return 2
        sys.setrecursionlimit(10000)
        run(argv[1], argv[2])
        return 0
    
    
    if __name__ == "__main__":
        sys.exit(main(sys.argv))

--------------------------------------------------------- feeder limits\n    def
load_feeder_limits(self):\n        lines =

----------------------------------------------------------- site state\n    def 
load_state(self):\n        with open(self.path(\"site_state.jsonl\"), 
encoding=\"utf-8\") as handle:\n            lines = [line for line in 
handle.read().splitlines() if line.strip()]\n        
self.rows[\"site_state.jsonl\"] = len(lines)\n        self.state = {}\n        
for line in lines:\n            entry = json.loads(line)\n            key = 
(entry[\"event_id\"], entry[\"site_id\"])\n            if key in self.sites:\n  
self.state[key] = entry\n\n    # 
-------------------------------------------------------------- waivers\n    def

---------------------------------------------------------------- floors\n    def
load_floors(self):\n        with open(self.path(\"segment_floors.json\"), 
encoding=\"utf-8\") as handle:\n            entries = 
json.load(handle)[\"floors\"]\n        self.rows[\"segment_floors.json\"] =

self.region_name(entry[\"region_code\"]),\n

------------------------------------------------------------ rate cards\n    def
load_rate_cards(self):\n        cards = 
ET.parse(self.path(\"rate_cards.xml\")).getroot().findall(\"card\")\n        
self.rows[\"rate_cards.xml\"] = len(cards)\n        self.cards = {}\n        for
card in cards:\n            key = (card.get(\"event\"), card.get(\"site\"))\n   
if key not in self.sites:\n                continue\n            override = 
card.find(\"override\")\n            start = card.find(\"base\") if override is 
None else override\n            self.cards[key] = (int(start.text), 
int(card.find(\"cap\").text))\n\n    # 
--------------------------------------------------------------- weights\n    def
load_weights(self):\n        records =

self.rows[\"recipient_weights.tsv\"] = len(records)\n        self.grant_weights 
= {}\n        for record in records:\n            key = (record[\"event_id\"],

----------------------------------------------------------------- pools\n    def
load_pools(self):\n        lines = read_record_lines(self.path(\"pools.txt\"))\n
self.rows[\"pools.txt\"] = len(lines)\n        self.pools = {}\n        for line
in lines:\n            key = (line[0:10].strip(), 
self.region_name(line[10:14]))\n            self.pools[key] = 
int(line[14:26])\n\n    # 
------------------------------------------------------------ allowances\n    def
load_allowances(self):\n        records = 
read_commented_delimited(self.path(\"site_allowances.tsv\"), \"\\t\")\n        
self.rows[\"site_allowances.tsv\"] = len(records)\n        self.allowance_ticks 
= {}\n        draws_from = {}\n        for record in records:\n            
site_id = record[\"site_id\"]\n            self.allowance_ticks[site_id] = 
int(record[\"allowance_ticks\"])\n            draws_from[site_id] = 
(record.get(\"draws_from\") or \"\").strip()\n        self.allowance_root = {}\n
for site_id in self.allowance_ticks:\n            chain = [site_id]\n           
current = site_id\n            while draws_from.get(current):\n                
current = draws_from[current]\n                chain.append(current)\n          
for node in chain:\n                self.allowance_root[node] = current\n\n    
def root_of(self, site_id):\n        return self.allowance_root.get(site_id, 
site_id)\n\n\ndef compliance_label(extract, key, site):\n    state = 
extract.state.get(key)\n    if state is not None:\n        if 
state[\"safety_hold\"]:\n            return \"SAFETY_HOLD\"\n        if 
state[\"cooldown\"]:\n            return \"COOLDOWN\"\n    waiver = 
extract.waivers.get(key)\n    if waiver is None or 
waiver[\"routine_certificate_pending\"] != \"Y\":\n        return \"CLEAR\"\n   
waiver_valid = (\n        waiver[\"waiver_status\"] == \"VALID\"\n        and 
waiver[\"waiver_signed\"] == \"Y\"\n        and site[\"critical\"]\n        and 
site[\"segment\"] == \"public\"\n    )\n    return \"CONTINUITY_WAIVED\" if 
waiver_valid else \"CERTIFICATE_PENDING\"\n\n\ndef choose_portfolio(groups, 
targets, ceilings, floors, objective, interval_count):\n    \"\"\"Pick the best 
feasible portfolio by section 5.\n\n    groups holds one list of (offer, segment
index) pairs per site with\n    candidate offers. Returns (selected offer ids 
sorted, tied portfolio count).\n    \"\"\"\n    target_ticks, shortfall_weight =
targets\n    relief_ceiling, recovery_ceiling = ceilings\n    cost_weight, 
recovery_weight = objective\n    segment_count = len(floors)\n    relief = [0] *
interval_count\n    recovery = [0] * interval_count\n    segment_relief = [0] * 
segment_count\n    chosen = []\n    best_key = None\n    best_ids = ()\n    tied
= 0\n\n    def evaluate(bid_cost):\n        nonlocal best_key, best_ids, tied\n 
weighted_shortfall = 0\n        overdelivery = 0\n        recovery_sum = 0\n    
for index in range(interval_count):\n            gap = target_ticks[index] - 
relief[index]\n            if gap > 0:\n                weighted_shortfall += 
shortfall_weight[index] * gap\n            elif gap < 0:\n                
overdelivery -= gap\n            recovery_sum += recovery[index]\n        
floor_deficit = 0\n        for index in range(segment_count):\n            
floor_ticks, priority_weight = floors[index]\n            deficit = floor_ticks 
- segment_relief[index]\n            if deficit > 0:\n                
floor_deficit += priority_weight * deficit\n        key = (\n            
weighted_shortfall,\n            floor_deficit,\n            cost_weight * 
bid_cost + recovery_weight * recovery_sum,\n            overdelivery,\n         
len(chosen),\n        )\n        if best_key is None or key < best_key:\n       
best_key = key\n            best_ids = tuple(sorted(chosen))\n            tied =
1\n        elif key == best_key:\n            tied += 1\n            ids = 
tuple(sorted(chosen))\n            if ids > best_ids:\n                best_ids 
= ids\n\n    def recurse(index, bid_cost):\n        if index == len(groups):\n  
evaluate(bid_cost)\n            return\n        recurse(index + 1, bid_cost)\n  
for offer, segment_index in groups[index]:\n            offer_relief = 
offer[\"relief\"]\n            offer_recovery = offer[\"recovery\"]\n           
feasible = True\n            for slot in range(interval_count):\n               
relief[slot] += offer_relief[slot]\n                recovery[slot] += 
offer_recovery[slot]\n                if (\n                    relief[slot] > 
relief_ceiling[slot]\n                    or recovery[slot] > 
recovery_ceiling[slot]\n                ):\n                    feasible = 
False\n            if feasible:\n                segment_relief[segment_index] 
+= offer[\"relief_total\"]\n                chosen.append(offer[\"offer_id\"])\n
recurse(index + 1, bid_cost + offer[\"bid_cost_cents\"])\n                
chosen.pop()\n                segment_relief[segment_index] -= 
offer[\"relief_total\"]\n            for slot in range(interval_count):\n       
relief[slot] -= offer_relief[slot]\n                recovery[slot] -= 
offer_recovery[slot]\n\n    recurse(0, 0)\n    return list(best_ids), 
tied\n\n\ndef largest_remainder(funded, weights):\n    \"\"\"Share funded cents 
in proportion to weights; returns shares, leftover, tie.\"\"\"\n    total = 
sum(weights.values())\n    shares = {}\n    remainders = {}\n    for 
beneficiary, weight in weights.items():\n        product = funded * weight\n    
shares[beneficiary] = product // total\n        remainders[beneficiary] = 
product % total\n    leftover = funded - sum(shares.values())\n    order = 
sorted(weights, reverse=True)\n    order.sort(key=lambda beneficiary: 
-remainders[beneficiary])\n    for beneficiary in order[:leftover]:\n        
shares[beneficiary] += 1\n    seen = defaultdict(int)\n    for remainder in 
remainders.values():\n        if remainder:\n            seen[remainder] += 1\n 
return shares, leftover, any(count >= 2 for count in seen.values())\n\n\ndef 
apportion(funded, weights, claim_per_weight, minimum):\n    \"\"\"Section 7: 
returns shares, allocated, unallocated, residual, residual tie.\"\"\"\n    
positive = {\n        beneficiary: weight for beneficiary, weight in 
weights.items() if weight > 0\n    }\n    if not positive:\n        return {}, 
0, funded, 0, False\n    claims = {\n        beneficiary: weight * 
claim_per_weight\n        for beneficiary, weight in positive.items()\n    }\n  
if sum(claims.values()) <= funded:\n        allocated = sum(claims.values())\n  
return claims, allocated, funded - allocated, 0, False\n    shares, residual, 
tie = largest_remainder(funded, positive)\n    below = [\n        beneficiary 
for beneficiary, cents in shares.items() if cents < minimum\n    ]\n    if 
len(below) == len(positive):\n        return {beneficiary: 0 for beneficiary in 
positive}, 0, funded, 0, False\n    if below:\n        keep = {\n            
beneficiary: weight\n            for beneficiary, weight in positive.items()\n  
if beneficiary not in below\n        }\n        shares, residual, tie = 
largest_remainder(funded, keep)\n        for beneficiary in below:\n            
shares[beneficiary] = 0\n    allocated = sum(shares.values())\n    return 
shares, allocated, funded - allocated, residual, tie\n\n\ndef derive(extract):\n
\"\"\"Work the season through in season order; returns per event-region 
results.\"\"\"\n    credit_denominator = 1000 * extract.millicents_per_cent\n\n 
site_rows = defaultdict(list)\n    for key in sorted(extract.sites):\n        
site = extract.sites[key]\n        site_rows[(site[\"event_id\"], 
site[\"region\"])].append(key)\n\n    labels = {key: compliance_label(extract, 
key, extract.sites[key]) for key in extract.sites}\n\n    effective_rate = {}\n 
cap_binding = {}\n    for key, site in extract.sites.items():\n        card = 
extract.cards.get(key)\n        if card is None:\n            continue\n        
rate, cap = card\n        rate += extract.segment_adder.get(site[\"segment\"], 
0)\n        rate += extract.events[site[\"event_id\"]][\"uplift\"]\n        
effective_rate[key] = min(rate, cap)\n        cap_binding[key] = rate > cap\n\n 
offers_of_site_row = defaultdict(list)\n    for offer_id in 
sorted(extract.offers):\n        offer = extract.offers[offer_id]\n        
offers_of_site_row[(offer[\"event_id\"], offer[\"site_id\"])].append(offer)\n\n 
segment_index = {name: index for index, name in 
enumerate(extract.segment_names)}\n\n    audit = {\n        
\"cap_binding_count\": 0,\n        \"residual_cents\": 0,\n        
\"residual_tie_pool_count\": 0,\n    }\n    quality = {\n        
\"ineligible_offer_count\": 0,\n        \"allowance_blocked_offer_count\": 0,\n 
\"empty_candidate_groups\": 0,\n        \"unallocated_pool_count\": 0,\n    }\n 
tie_decisions = []\n    drawn = defaultdict(int)\n    carried = 
defaultdict(int)\n    results = {}\n\n    for event_id in 
extract.season_order:\n        event = extract.events[event_id]\n        
interval_count = event[\"interval_count\"]\n\n        # Candidate offers of the 
whole event, before any of its own draws.\n        candidates = {region: 
defaultdict(list) for region in extract.region_names}\n        for region in 
extract.region_names:\n            for key in site_rows[(event_id, region)]:\n  
site = extract.sites[key]\n                state = extract.state.get(key)\n     
can_dispatch = state is not None and state[\"dispatch_authorized\"]\n           
root = extract.root_of(site[\"site_id\"])\n                remaining = 
extract.allowance_ticks.get(root, 0) - drawn[root]\n                for offer in
offers_of_site_row[key]:\n                    if not can_dispatch:\n            
quality[\"ineligible_offer_count\"] += 1\n                    elif 
offer[\"relief_total\"] > remaining:\n                        
quality[\"allowance_blocked_offer_count\"] += 1\n                    else:\n    
candidates[region][site[\"site_id\"]].append(offer)\n\n        for region in 
extract.region_names:\n            groups = []\n            
candidate_offer_count = 0\n            for site_id in 
sorted(candidates[region]):\n                offers = 
candidates[region][site_id]\n                segment = extract.sites[(event_id, 
site_id)][\"segment\"]\n                groups.append([(offer, 
segment_index[segment]) for offer in offers])\n                
candidate_offer_count += len(offers)\n            if candidate_offer_count == 
0:\n                quality[\"empty_candidate_groups\"] += 1\n\n            
targets = extract.target_of(event_id, region)\n            floors = [\n         
extract.floor_of(event_id, region, segment)\n                for segment in 
extract.segment_names\n            ]\n            selected_ids, tied = 
choose_portfolio(\n                groups,\n                targets,\n          
extract.ceiling_of(event_id, region),\n                floors,\n                
(event[\"cost_weight\"], event[\"recovery_weight\"]),\n                
interval_count,\n            )\n            if tied >= 2:\n                
tie_decisions.append(\n                    {\n                        
\"event_id\": event_id,\n                        \"region\": region,\n          
\"tied_portfolio_count\": tied,\n                        \"selected_offer_ids\":
list(selected_ids),\n                    }\n                )\n\n            
relief = [0] * interval_count\n            recovery = [0] * interval_count\n    
bid_cost_cents = 0\n            credit_cents = 0\n            offer_credits = 
{}\n            for offer_id in selected_ids:\n                offer = 
extract.offers[offer_id]\n                key = (offer[\"event_id\"], 
offer[\"site_id\"])\n                bid_cost_cents += 
offer[\"bid_cost_cents\"]\n                rate = effective_rate.get(key, 0)\n  
credit = 0\n                for slot in range(interval_count):\n                
relief[slot] += offer[\"relief\"][slot]\n                    recovery[slot] += 
offer[\"recovery\"][slot]\n                    credited = 
offer[\"relief\"][slot] - offer[\"recovery\"][slot]\n                    if 
credited > 0:\n                        credit += half_even(\n                   
credited * extract.wh_per_tick * rate, credit_denominator\n                     
)\n                offer_credits[offer_id] = credit\n                
credit_cents += credit\n                if cap_binding.get(key):\n              
audit[\"cap_binding_count\"] += 1\n                
drawn[extract.root_of(offer[\"site_id\"])] += offer[\"relief_total\"]\n\n       
pool_cents = extract.pools.get((event_id, region), 0)\n            funded_cents 
= max(0, pool_cents + carried[region] - credit_cents)\n            weights = 
defaultdict(int)\n            recipients = 0\n            for key in 
site_rows[(event_id, region)]:\n                if key not in 
extract.grant_weights:\n                    continue\n                recipients
+= 1\n                weight = extract.grant_weights[key]\n                if 
labels[key] == \"CONTINUITY_WAIVED\":\n                    weight *= 2\n        
weights[extract.sites[key][\"beneficiary_id\"]] += weight\n            shares, 
allocated, unallocated, residual, tie = apportion(\n                
funded_cents,\n                dict(weights),\n                
extract.claim_cents_per_weight,\n                extract.minimum_grant_cents,\n 
)\n            if recipients == 0 or sum(weights.values()) == 0:\n              
quality[\"unallocated_pool_count\"] += 1\n            audit[\"residual_cents\"] 
+= residual\n            if tie:\n                
audit[\"residual_tie_pool_count\"] += 1\n            carried[region] = 
unallocated\n\n            counts = {label: 0 for label in LABELS}\n            
for key in site_rows[(event_id, region)]:\n                counts[labels[key]] 
+= 1\n\n            results[(event_id, region)] = {\n                
\"candidate_offer_count\": candidate_offer_count,\n                
\"selected_ids\": list(selected_ids),\n                \"offer_credits\": 
offer_credits,\n                \"relief\": relief,\n                
\"recovery\": recovery,\n                \"targets\": targets[0],\n             
\"bid_cost_cents\": bid_cost_cents,\n                \"credit_cents\": 
credit_cents,\n                \"grant_pool_cents\": pool_cents,\n              
\"grant_funded_cents\": funded_cents,\n                
\"grant_allocated_cents\": allocated,\n                
\"grant_unallocated_cents\": unallocated,\n                \"grants\": shares,\n
\"compliance_counts\": counts,\n            }\n\n    return results, labels, 
tie_decisions, audit, quality\n\n\ndef dump(path, document):\n    with 
open(path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as handle:\n        json.dump(document, 
handle, sort_keys=True, indent=1)\n        handle.write(\"\\n\")\n\n\ndef 
region_document(extract, results, region):\n    events = []\n    totals = {key: 
0 for key in REGION_TOTAL_KEYS}\n    for event_id in extract.event_ids:\n       
result = results[(event_id, region)]\n        intervals = []\n        for slot 
in range(extract.events[event_id][\"interval_count\"]):\n            target = 
result[\"targets\"][slot]\n            relief = result[\"relief\"][slot]\n      
intervals.append(\n                {\n                    \"interval\": slot + 
1,\n                    \"target_ticks\": target,\n                    
\"relief_ticks\": relief,\n                    \"recovery_ticks\": 
result[\"recovery\"][slot],\n                    \"shortfall_ticks\": max(0, 
target - relief),\n                    \"overdelivery_ticks\": max(0, relief - 
target),\n                }\n            )\n        events.append(\n            
{\n                \"event_id\": event_id,\n                
\"candidate_offer_count\": result[\"candidate_offer_count\"],\n                
\"selected_offer_ids\": result[\"selected_ids\"],\n                
\"intervals\": intervals,\n                \"bid_cost_cents\": 
result[\"bid_cost_cents\"],\n                \"credit_cents\": 
result[\"credit_cents\"],\n                \"grant_pool_cents\": 
result[\"grant_pool_cents\"],\n                \"grant_funded_cents\": 
result[\"grant_funded_cents\"],\n                \"grant_allocated_cents\": 
result[\"grant_allocated_cents\"],\n                \"grant_unallocated_cents\":
result[\"grant_unallocated_cents\"],\n                \"grants\": [\n           
{\"beneficiary_id\": beneficiary, \"cents\": cents}\n                    for 
beneficiary, cents in sorted(result[\"grants\"].items())\n                ],\n  
\"compliance_counts\": result[\"compliance_counts\"],\n            }\n        
)\n        totals[\"event_count\"] += 1\n        
totals[\"candidate_offer_count\"] += result[\"candidate_offer_count\"]\n        
totals[\"selected_offer_count\"] += len(result[\"selected_ids\"])\n        
totals[\"relief_ticks\"] += sum(result[\"relief\"])\n        
totals[\"recovery_ticks\"] += sum(result[\"recovery\"])\n        
totals[\"shortfall_ticks\"] += sum(item[\"shortfall_ticks\"] for item in 
intervals)\n        totals[\"overdelivery_ticks\"] += sum(\n            
item[\"overdelivery_ticks\"] for item in intervals\n        )\n        for key 
in (\n            \"bid_cost_cents\",\n            \"credit_cents\",\n          
\"grant_pool_cents\",\n            \"grant_funded_cents\",\n            
\"grant_allocated_cents\",\n            \"grant_unallocated_cents\",\n        
):\n            totals[key] += result[key]\n    return {\"region\": region, 
\"events\": events, \"totals\": totals}\n\n\ndef segment_document(extract, 
results, segment):\n    entries = []\n    totals = {key: 0 for key in 
SEGMENT_TOTAL_KEYS}\n    for event_id in extract.event_ids:\n        for region 
in extract.region_names:\n            result = results[(event_id, region)]\n    
offer_ids = []\n            relief_ticks = 0\n            recovery_ticks = 0\n  
bid_cost_cents = 0\n            credit_cents = 0\n            for offer_id in 
result[\"selected_ids\"]:\n                offer = extract.offers[offer_id]\n   
site = extract.sites[(offer[\"event_id\"], offer[\"site_id\"])]\n               
if site[\"segment\"] != segment:\n                    continue\n                
offer_ids.append(offer_id)\n                relief_ticks += 
offer[\"relief_total\"]\n                recovery_ticks += 
offer[\"recovery_total\"]\n                bid_cost_cents += 
offer[\"bid_cost_cents\"]\n                credit_cents += 
result[\"offer_credits\"][offer_id]\n            floor_ticks = 
extract.floor_of(event_id, region, segment)[0]\n            if floor_ticks == 
0:\n                attainment = 10000\n            else:\n                
attainment = min(10000, half_up(10000 * relief_ticks, floor_ticks))\n           
entries.append(\n                {\n                    \"event_id\": 
event_id,\n                    \"region\": region,\n                    
\"selected_offer_ids\": sorted(offer_ids),\n                    
\"relief_ticks\": relief_ticks,\n                    \"recovery_ticks\": 
recovery_ticks,\n                    \"bid_cost_cents\": bid_cost_cents,\n      
\"credit_cents\": credit_cents,\n                    \"floor_ticks\": 
floor_ticks,\n                    \"floor_attainment_bp\": attainment,\n        
}\n            )\n            totals[\"selected_offer_count\"] += 
len(offer_ids)\n            totals[\"relief_ticks\"] += relief_ticks\n          
totals[\"recovery_ticks\"] += recovery_ticks\n            
totals[\"bid_cost_cents\"] += bid_cost_cents\n            
totals[\"credit_cents\"] += credit_cents\n            totals[\"floor_ticks\"] +=
floor_ticks\n    return {\"segment\": segment, \"event_regions\": entries, 
\"totals\": totals}\n\n\ndef write_outputs(extract, out_dir, results, labels, 
tie_decisions, audit, quality):\n    os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)\n\n

\"residual_tie_pool_count\": audit[\"residual_tie_pool_count\"],\n        },\n  
)\n\n    document = dict(quality)\n    document[\"rows\"] = dict(extract.rows)\n

segment in extract.segment_names\n        if extract.floor_of(event_id, region, 
segment)[0] == 0\n    )\n    dump(os.path.join(out_dir, \"data_quality.json\"), 
document)\n\n\ndef main(argv):\n    if len(argv) != 3:\n        
sys.stderr.write(\"usage: derive_report.py DATA_DIR OUT_DIR\\n\")\n        
return 2\n    extract = Extract(argv[1])\n    results, labels, tie_decisions, 
audit, quality = derive(extract)\n    write_outputs(extract, argv[2], results, 
labels, tie_decisions, audit, quality)\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == 
\"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main(sys.argv))\n"}

Reasoning:
I need to clean up the draft before rewriting the file, removing leftover 
placeholder code and fixing the mismatched function signature for 
choose_portfolio where the arguments don't line up correctly.

I'm double-checking the ordering of operations: remaining values for candidates 
get computed before the region optimization loop for an event, so consumption 
updates don't leak into candidate building for the same event — that looks 
correct. I'm also verifying that ineligible_offer_count correctly tallies 
non-orphan offers whose site can't dispatch, scanning across all site rows in 
the event's regions.

The orphan-offer logic also checks out: a profile row referencing an offer that 
only exists in offers.csv as an orphan is still counted as "kept" for duplicate 
detection even though the underlying offer isn't stored, matching the stated 
rule that it's not counted in skip categories. I'm also confirming that the 
relief array sizing uses the event count, which should be safe given event_ids 
are guaranteed to exist. Now I'm checking the pending/waiver label logic — if 
there's no waiver row, pending defaults to False, resulting in a CLEAR label, 
which seems right.

For missing site state, I'll treat it as no dispatch capability and no safety 
hold since the schema guarantees one state line per site. For CONTINUITY_WAIVED,
the rule applies when a site's routine certificate is pending and it has a valid
continuity waiver, which matches my implementation. For rate cards, a missing 
card defaults to zero since the schema guarantees one card per site row, and for
segment codes, an unknown code results in None, which would fail fast on lookup 
— that seems like an acceptable behavior since the schema should always provide 
valid segment codes. Time to finalize and rewrite the file cleanly.
