    #!/usr/bin/env python3
    """Derive the district-heat flexibility programme reports from one season 
extract.
    
    Usage: python3 derive_report.py DATA_DIR OUT_DIR
    """
    
    import configparser
    import csv
    import json
    import os
    import sys
    import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
    from collections import defaultdict
    from datetime import datetime
    
    LABELS = (
        "SAFETY_HOLD",
        "COOLDOWN",
        "CONTINUITY_WAIVED",
        "CERTIFICATE_PENDING",
        "CLEAR",
    )
    
    REGION_TOTAL_KEYS = (
        "event_count",
        "candidate_offer_count",
        "selected_offer_count",
        "relief_ticks",
        "recovery_ticks",
        "shortfall_ticks",
        "overdelivery_ticks",
        "bid_cost_cents",
        "credit_cents",
        "grant_pool_cents",
        "grant_funded_cents",
        "grant_allocated_cents",
        "grant_unallocated_cents",
    )
    
    SEGMENT_TOTAL_KEYS = (
        "selected_offer_count",
        "relief_ticks",
        "recovery_ticks",
        "bid_cost_cents",
        "credit_cents",
        "floor_ticks",
    )
    
    
    def half_up(num, den):
        return (2 * num + den) // (2 * den)
    
    
    def half_even(num, den):
        quotient, rest = divmod(num, den)
        twice = 2 * rest
        if twice > den or (twice == den and quotient % 2):
            quotient += 1
        return quotient
    
    
    def read_delimited(path, delimiter):
        with open(path, encoding="utf-8", newline="") as handle:
            return list(csv.DictReader(handle, delimiter=delimiter))
    
    
    def read_commented_delimited(path, delimiter):
        with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as handle:
            lines = [
                line
                for line in handle.read().splitlines()
                if line.strip() and not line.startswith("#")
            ]
        return list(csv.DictReader(lines, delimiter=delimiter))
    
    
    def read_record_lines(path):
        with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as handle:
            return [
                line
                for line in handle.read().splitlines()
                if line.strip() and not line.startswith("#")
            ]
    
    
    class Extract:
        """Everything one season extract says, parsed into report-ready form."""
    
        def __init__(self, data_dir):
            self.dir = data_dir
            self.rows = {}
            self.load_config()
            self.load_events()
            self.load_sites()
            self.load_offers()
            self.load_profiles()
            self.load_targets()
            self.load_feeder_limits()
            self.load_state()
            self.load_waivers()
            self.load_floors()
            self.load_rate_cards()
            self.load_weights()
            self.load_pools()
            self.load_allowances()
            self.rows["export_manifest.tsv"] = len(
                read_commented_delimited(self.path("export_manifest.tsv"), "\t")
            )
    
        def path(self, name):
            return os.path.join(self.dir, name)
    
        def ticks(self, wh):
            return half_up(wh, self.wh_per_tick)
    
        def region_name(self, code):
            return self.region_of_code.get(code.strip().lower())
    
        def segment_name(self, code):
            return self.segment_of_code.get(code.strip().lower())
    
        # ---------------------------------------------------------------- 
config
        def load_config(self):
            cfg = configparser.ConfigParser()
            with open(self.path("program.ini"), encoding="utf-8") as handle:
                cfg.read_file(handle)
            self.wh_per_tick = cfg.getint("units", "wh_per_tick")
            self.millicents_per_cent = cfg.getint("units", 
"millicents_per_cent")
            self.region_of_code = dict(cfg["region_codes"])
            self.segment_of_code = dict(cfg["segment_codes"])
            self.region_names = sorted(set(self.region_of_code.values()))
            self.segment_names = sorted(set(self.segment_of_code.values()))
            adders = dict(cfg["segment_rate_adders"]) if cfg.has_section(
                "segment_rate_adders"
            ) else {}
            self.segment_adder = {
                name: int(adders.get(name.lower(), 0)) for name in 
self.segment_names
            }
            self.claim_cents_per_weight = cfg.getint("grants", 
"claim_cents_per_weight")
            self.minimum_grant_cents = cfg.getint("grants", 
"minimum_grant_cents")
    
        # ---------------------------------------------------------------- 
events
        def load_events(self):
            with open(self.path("events.json"), encoding="utf-8") as handle:
                raw = json.load(handle)
            self.rows["events.json"] = len(raw)
            self.events = {}
            for entry in raw:
                self.events[entry["event_id"]] = {
                    "event_id": entry["event_id"],
                    "interval_count": int(entry["interval_count"]),
                    "cost_weight": int(entry["objective"]["cost_weight"]),
                    "recovery_weight": 
int(entry["objective"]["recovery_weight"]),
                    "uplift": int(entry["uplift_millicents_per_kwh"]),
                    "start": datetime.fromisoformat(entry["dispatch_start"]),
                }
            self.event_ids = sorted(self.events)
            # Season order: ascending start instant, events sharing an instant
            # reached in descending event_id (section 2.4).
            self.season_order = sorted(
                sorted(self.event_ids, reverse=True),
                key=lambda event_id: self.events[event_id]["start"],
            )
    
        # ----------------------------------------------------------------- 
sites
        def load_sites(self):
            records = read_delimited(self.path("sites.csv"), ",")
            self.rows["sites.csv"] = len(records)
            self.sites = {}
            self.unknown_region_site_rows = 0
            for record in records:
                region = self.region_name(record["region_code"])
                if region is None:
                    self.unknown_region_site_rows += 1
                    continue
                self.sites[(record["event_id"], record["site_id"])] = {
                    "event_id": record["event_id"],
                    "site_id": record["site_id"],
                    "region": region,
                    "segment": self.segment_name(record["segment_code"]),
                    "beneficiary_id": record["beneficiary_id"],
                    "critical": record["critical"] == "Y",
                }
    
        # ---------------------------------------------------------------- 
offers
        def load_offers(self):
            records = read_delimited(self.path("offers.csv"), ",")
            self.rows["offers.csv"] = len(records)
            self.offers = {}
            self.offer_event = {}
            self.orphan_offer_rows = 0
            for record in records:
                self.offer_event[record["offer_id"]] = record["event_id"]
                key = (record["event_id"], record["site_id"])
                if key not in self.sites:
                    self.orphan_offer_rows += 1
                    continue
                count = self.events[record["event_id"]]["interval_count"]
                self.offers[record["offer_id"]] = {
                    "offer_id": record["offer_id"],
                    "event_id": record["event_id"],
                    "site_id": record["site_id"],
                    "bid_cost_cents": int(record["bid_cost_cents"]),
                    "relief": [0] * count,
                    "recovery": [0] * count,
                    "relief_total": 0,
                    "recovery_total": 0,
                }
    
        # -------------------------------------------------------------- 
profiles
        def load_profiles(self):
            records = read_delimited(self.path("profiles.tsv"), "\t")
            self.rows["profiles.tsv"] = len(records)
            self.orphan_profile_rows = 0
            self.out_of_range_profile_rows = 0
            self.duplicate_profile_rows = 0
            kept = set()
            for record in records:
                offer_id = record["offer_id"]
                event_id = self.offer_event.get(offer_id)
                if event_id is None:
                    self.orphan_profile_rows += 1
                    continue
                interval = int(record["interval"])
                if interval < 1 or interval > 
self.events[event_id]["interval_count"]:
                    self.out_of_range_profile_rows += 1
                    continue
                if (offer_id, interval) in kept:
                    self.duplicate_profile_rows += 1
                    continue
                kept.add((offer_id, interval))
                offer = self.offers.get(offer_id)
                if offer is None:
                    continue
                multiplier = int(record["register_multiplier"])
                relief = self.ticks(int(record["relief_wh"]) * multiplier)
                recovery = self.ticks(int(record["recovery_wh"]) * multiplier)
                offer["relief"][interval - 1] = relief
                offer["recovery"][interval - 1] = recovery
                offer["relief_total"] += relief
                offer["recovery_total"] += recovery
    
        # --------------------------------------------------------------- 
targets
        def load_targets(self):
            records = read_delimited(self.path("targets.tsv"), "\t")
            self.rows["targets.tsv"] = len(records)
            self.targets = {}
            for record in records:
                event_id = record["event_id"]
                key = (event_id, self.region_name(record["region_code"]))
                count = self.events[event_id]["interval_count"]
                slot = self.targets.setdefault(key, ([0] * count, [0] * count))
                interval = int(record["interval"])
                slot[0][interval - 1] = self.ticks(int(record["target_wh"]))
                slot[1][interval - 1] = int(record["shortfall_weight"])
    
        def target_of(self, event_id, region):
            count = self.events[event_id]["interval_count"]
            return self.targets.get((event_id, region), ([0] * count, [0] * 
count))
    
        # --------------------------------------------------------- feeder 
limits
        def load_feeder_limits(self):
            lines = read_record_lines(self.path("feeder_limits.txt"))
            self.rows["feeder_limits.txt"] = len(lines)
            self.ceilings = {}
            for line in lines:
                event_id = line[0:10].strip()
                key = (event_id, self.region_name(line[10:14]))
                count = self.events[event_id]["interval_count"]
                slot = self.ceilings.setdefault((key), ([0] * count, [0] * 
count))
                interval = int(line[14:17])
                slot[0][interval - 1] = self.ticks(int(line[17:27]))
                slot[1][interval - 1] = self.ticks(int(line[27:37]))
    
        def ceiling_of(self, event_id, region):
            count = self.events[event_id]["interval_count"]
            return self.ceilings.get((event_id, region), ([0] * count, [0] * 
count))
    
        # ----------------------------------------------------------- site state
        def load_state(self):
            with open(self.path("site_state.jsonl"), encoding="utf-8") as 
handle:
                lines = [line for line in handle.read().splitlines() if 
line.strip()]
            self.rows["site_state.jsonl"] = len(lines)
            self.state = {}
            for line in lines:
                entry = json.loads(line)
                key = (entry["event_id"], entry["site_id"])
                if key in self.sites:
                    self.state[key] = entry
    
        # -------------------------------------------------------------- waivers
        def load_waivers(self):
            records = read_delimited(self.path("continuity_waivers.csv"), ",")
            self.rows["continuity_waivers.csv"] = len(records)
            self.waiver_signed_missing = 0
            self.waivers = {}
            for record in records:
                if record["waiver_signed"].strip() == "":
                    self.waiver_signed_missing += 1
                key = (record["event_id"], record["site_id"])
                if key in self.sites:
                    self.waivers[key] = record
    
        # ---------------------------------------------------------------- 
floors
        def load_floors(self):
            with open(self.path("segment_floors.json"), encoding="utf-8") as 
handle:
                entries = json.load(handle)["floors"]
            self.rows["segment_floors.json"] = len(entries)
            self.floors = {}
            for entry in entries:
                key = (
                    entry["event_id"],
                    self.region_name(entry["region_code"]),
                    self.segment_name(entry["segment_code"]),
                )
                self.floors[key] = (
                    self.ticks(int(entry["floor_wh"])),
                    int(entry["priority_weight"]),
                )
    
        def floor_of(self, event_id, region, segment):
            return self.floors.get((event_id, region, segment), (0, 0))
    
        # ------------------------------------------------------------ rate 
cards
        def load_rate_cards(self):
            cards = 
ET.parse(self.path("rate_cards.xml")).getroot().findall("card")
            self.rows["rate_cards.xml"] = len(cards)
            self.cards = {}
            for card in cards:
                key = (card.get("event"), card.get("site"))
                if key not in self.sites:
                    continue
                override = card.find("override")
                start = card.find("base") if override is None else override
                self.cards[key] = (int(start.text), int(card.find("cap").text))
    
        # --------------------------------------------------------------- 
weights
        def load_weights(self):
            records = read_delimited(self.path("recipient_weights.tsv"), "\t")
            self.rows["recipient_weights.tsv"] = len(records)
            self.grant_weights = {}
            for record in records:
                key = (record["event_id"], record["site_id"])
                if key in self.sites:
                    self.grant_weights[key] = int(record["grant_weight"])
    
        # ----------------------------------------------------------------- 
pools
        def load_pools(self):
            lines = read_record_lines(self.path("pools.txt"))
            self.rows["pools.txt"] = len(lines)
            self.pools = {}
            for line in lines:
                key = (line[0:10].strip(), self.region_name(line[10:14]))
                self.pools[key] = int(line[14:26])
    
        # ------------------------------------------------------------ 
allowances
        def load_allowances(self):
            records = read_commented_delimited(self.path("site_allowances.tsv"),
"\t")
            self.rows["site_allowances.tsv"] = len(records)
            self.allowance_ticks = {}
            draws_from = {}
            for record in records:
                site_id = record["site_id"]
                self.allowance_ticks[site_id] = int(record["allowance_ticks"])
                draws_from[site_id] = (record.get("draws_from") or "").strip()
            self.allowance_root = {}
            for site_id in self.allowance_ticks:
                chain = [site_id]
                current = site_id
                while draws_from.get(current):
                    current = draws_from[current]
                    chain.append(current)
                for node in chain:
                    self.allowance_root[node] = current
    
        def root_of(self, site_id):
            return self.allowance_root.get(site_id, site_id)
    
    
    def compliance_label(extract, key, site):
        state = extract.state.get(key)
        if state is not None:
            if state["safety_hold"]:
                return "SAFETY_HOLD"
            if state["cooldown"]:
                return "COOLDOWN"
        waiver = extract.waivers.get(key)
        if waiver is None or waiver["routine_certificate_pending"] != "Y":
            return "CLEAR"
        waiver_valid = (
            waiver["waiver_status"] == "VALID"
            and waiver["waiver_signed"] == "Y"
            and site["critical"]
            and site["segment"] == "public"
        )
        return "CONTINUITY_WAIVED" if waiver_valid else "CERTIFICATE_PENDING"
    
    
    def choose_portfolio(groups, targets, ceilings, floors, objective, 
interval_count):
        """Pick the best feasible portfolio by section 5.
    
        groups holds one list of (offer, segment index) pairs per site with
        candidate offers. Returns (selected offer ids sorted, tied portfolio 
count).
        """
        target_ticks, shortfall_weight = targets
        relief_ceiling, recovery_ceiling = ceilings
        cost_weight, recovery_weight = objective
        segment_count = len(floors)
        relief = [0] * interval_count
        recovery = [0] * interval_count
        segment_relief = [0] * segment_count
        chosen = []
        best_key = None
        best_ids = ()
        tied = 0
    
        def evaluate(bid_cost):
            nonlocal best_key, best_ids, tied
            weighted_shortfall = 0
            overdelivery = 0
            recovery_sum = 0
            for index in range(interval_count):
                gap = target_ticks[index] - relief[index]
                if gap > 0:
                    weighted_shortfall += shortfall_weight[index] * gap
                elif gap < 0:
                    overdelivery -= gap
                recovery_sum += recovery[index]
            floor_deficit = 0
            for index in range(segment_count):
                floor_ticks, priority_weight = floors[index]
                deficit = floor_ticks - segment_relief[index]
                if deficit > 0:
                    floor_deficit += priority_weight * deficit
            key = (
                weighted_shortfall,
                floor_deficit,
                cost_weight * bid_cost + recovery_weight * recovery_sum,
                overdelivery,
                len(chosen),
            )
            if best_key is None or key < best_key:
                best_key = key
                best_ids = tuple(sorted(chosen))
                tied = 1
            elif key == best_key:
                tied += 1
                ids = tuple(sorted(chosen))
                if ids > best_ids:
                    best_ids = ids
    
        def recurse(index, bid_cost):
            if index == len(groups):
                evaluate(bid_cost)
                return
            recurse(index + 1, bid_cost)
            for offer, segment_index in groups[index]:
                offer_relief = offer["relief"]
                offer_recovery = offer["recovery"]
                feasible = True
                for slot in range(interval_count):
                    relief[slot] += offer_relief[slot]
                    recovery[slot] += offer_recovery[slot]
                    if (
                        relief[slot] > relief_ceiling[slot]
                        or recovery[slot] > recovery_ceiling[slot]
                    ):
                        feasible = False
                if feasible:
                    segment_relief[segment_index] += offer["relief_total"]
                    chosen.append(offer["offer_id"])
                    recurse(index + 1, bid_cost + offer["bid_cost_cents"])
                    chosen.pop()
                    segment_relief[segment_index] -= offer["relief_total"]
                for slot in range(interval_count):
                    relief[slot] -= offer_relief[slot]
                    recovery[slot] -= offer_recovery[slot]
    
        recurse(0, 0)
        return list(best_ids), tied
    
    
    def largest_remainder(funded, weights):
        """Share funded cents in proportion to weights; returns shares, 
leftover, tie."""
        total = sum(weights.values())
        shares = {}
        remainders = {}
        for beneficiary, weight in weights.items():
            product = funded * weight
            shares[beneficiary] = product // total
            remainders[beneficiary] = product % total
        leftover = funded - sum(shares.values())
        order = sorted(weights, reverse=True)
        order.sort(key=lambda beneficiary: -remainders[beneficiary])
        for beneficiary in order[:leftover]:
            shares[beneficiary] += 1
        seen = defaultdict(int)
        for remainder in remainders.values():
            if remainder:
                seen[remainder] += 1
        return shares, leftover, any(count >= 2 for count in seen.values())
    
    
    def apportion(funded, weights, claim_per_weight, minimum):
        """Section 7: returns shares, allocated, unallocated, residual, residual
tie."""
        positive = {
            beneficiary: weight for beneficiary, weight in weights.items() if 
weight > 0
        }
        if not positive:
            return {}, 0, funded, 0, False
        claims = {
            beneficiary: weight * claim_per_weight
            for beneficiary, weight in positive.items()
        }
        if sum(claims.values()) <= funded:
            allocated = sum(claims.values())
            return claims, allocated, funded - allocated, 0, False
        shares, residual, tie = largest_remainder(funded, positive)
        below = [
            beneficiary for beneficiary, cents in shares.items() if cents < 
minimum
        ]
        if len(below) == len(positive):
            return {beneficiary: 0 for beneficiary in positive}, 0, funded, 0, 
False
        if below:
            keep = {
                beneficiary: weight
                for beneficiary, weight in positive.items()
                if beneficiary not in below
            }
            shares, residual, tie = largest_remainder(funded, keep)
            for beneficiary in below:
                shares[beneficiary] = 0
        allocated = sum(shares.values())
        return shares, allocated, funded - allocated, residual, tie
    
    
    def derive(extract):
        """Work the season through in season order; returns per event-region 
results."""
        credit_denominator = 1000 * extract.millicents_per_cent
    
        site_rows = defaultdict(list)
        for key in sorted(extract.sites):
            site = extract.sites[key]
            site_rows[(site["event_id"], site["region"])].append(key)
    
        labels = {key: compliance_label(extract, key, extract.sites[key]) for 
key in extract.sites}
    
        effective_rate = {}
        cap_binding = {}
        for key, site in extract.sites.items():
            card = extract.cards.get(key)
            if card is None:
                continue
            rate, cap = card
            rate += extract.segment_adder.get(site["segment"], 0)
            rate += extract.events[site["event_id"]]["uplift"]
            effective_rate[key] = min(rate, cap)
            cap_binding[key] = rate > cap
    
        offers_of_site_row = defaultdict(list)
        for offer_id in sorted(extract.offers):
            offer = extract.offers[offer_id]
            offers_of_site_row[(offer["event_id"], 
offer["site_id"])].append(offer)
    
        segment_index = {name: index for index, name in 
enumerate(extract.segment_names)}
    
        audit = {
            "cap_binding_count": 0,
            "residual_cents": 0,
            "residual_tie_pool_count": 0,
        }
        quality = {
            "ineligible_offer_count": 0,
            "allowance_blocked_offer_count": 0,
            "empty_candidate_groups": 0,
            "unallocated_pool_count": 0,
        }
        tie_decisions = []
        drawn = defaultdict(int)
        carried = defaultdict(int)
        results = {}
    
        for event_id in extract.season_order:
            event = extract.events[event_id]
            interval_count = event["interval_count"]
    
            # Candidate offers of the whole event, before any of its own draws.
            candidates = {region: defaultdict(list) for region in 
extract.region_names}
            for region in extract.region_names:
                for key in site_rows[(event_id, region)]:
                    site = extract.sites[key]
                    state = extract.state.get(key)
                    can_dispatch = state is not None and 
state["dispatch_authorized"]
                    root = extract.root_of(site["site_id"])
                    remaining = extract.allowance_ticks.get(root, 0) - 
drawn[root]
                    for offer in offers_of_site_row[key]:
                        if not can_dispatch:
                            quality["ineligible_offer_count"] += 1
                        elif offer["relief_total"] > remaining:
                            quality["allowance_blocked_offer_count"] += 1
                        else:
                            candidates[region][site["site_id"]].append(offer)
    
            for region in extract.region_names:
                groups = []
                candidate_offer_count = 0
                for site_id in sorted(candidates[region]):
                    offers = candidates[region][site_id]
                    segment = extract.sites[(event_id, site_id)]["segment"]
                    groups.append([(offer, segment_index[segment]) for offer in 
offers])
                    candidate_offer_count += len(offers)
                if candidate_offer_count == 0:
                    quality["empty_candidate_groups"] += 1
    
                targets = extract.target_of(event_id, region)
                floors = [
                    extract.floor_of(event_id, region, segment)
                    for segment in extract.segment_names
                ]
                selected_ids, tied = choose_portfolio(
                    groups,
                    targets,
                    extract.ceiling_of(event_id, region),
                    floors,
                    (event["cost_weight"], event["recovery_weight"]),
                    interval_count,
                )
                if tied >= 2:
                    tie_decisions.append(
                        {
                            "event_id": event_id,
                            "region": region,
                            "tied_portfolio_count": tied,
                            "selected_offer_ids": list(selected_ids),
                        }
                    )
    
                relief = [0] * interval_count
                recovery = [0] * interval_count
                bid_cost_cents = 0
                credit_cents = 0
                offer_credits = {}
                for offer_id in selected_ids:
                    offer = extract.offers[offer_id]
                    key = (offer["event_id"], offer["site_id"])
                    bid_cost_cents += offer["bid_cost_cents"]
                    rate = effective_rate.get(key, 0)
                    credit = 0
                    for slot in range(interval_count):
                        relief[slot] += offer["relief"][slot]
                        recovery[slot] += offer["recovery"][slot]
                        credited = offer["relief"][slot] - 
offer["recovery"][slot]
                        if credited > 0:
                            credit += half_even(
                                credited * extract.wh_per_tick * rate, 
credit_denominator
                            )
                    offer_credits[offer_id] = credit
                    credit_cents += credit
                    if cap_binding.get(key):
                        audit["cap_binding_count"] += 1
                    drawn[extract.root_of(offer["site_id"])] += 
offer["relief_total"]
    
                pool_cents = extract.pools.get((event_id, region), 0)
                funded_cents = max(0, pool_cents + carried[region] - 
credit_cents)
                weights = defaultdict(int)
                recipients = 0
                for key in site_rows[(event_id, region)]:
                    if key not in extract.grant_weights:
                        continue
                    recipients += 1
                    weight = extract.grant_weights[key]
                    if labels[key] == "CONTINUITY_WAIVED":
                        weight *= 2
                    weights[extract.sites[key]["beneficiary_id"]] += weight
                shares, allocated, unallocated, residual, tie = apportion(
                    funded_cents,
                    dict(weights),
                    extract.claim_cents_per_weight,
                    extract.minimum_grant_cents,
                )
                if recipients == 0 or sum(weights.values()) == 0:
                    quality["unallocated_pool_count"] += 1
                audit["residual_cents"] += residual
                if tie:
                    audit["residual_tie_pool_count"] += 1
                carried[region] = unallocated
    
                counts = {label: 0 for label in LABELS}
                for key in site_rows[(event_id, region)]:
                    counts[labels[key]] += 1
    
                results[(event_id, region)] = {
                    "candidate_offer_count": candidate_offer_count,
                    "selected_ids": list(selected_ids),
                    "offer_credits": offer_credits,
                    "relief": relief,
                    "recovery": recovery,
                    "targets": targets[0],
                    "bid_cost_cents": bid_cost_cents,
                    "credit_cents": credit_cents,
                    "grant_pool_cents": pool_cents,
                    "grant_funded_cents": funded_cents,
                    "grant_allocated_cents": allocated,
                    "grant_unallocated_cents": unallocated,
                    "grants": shares,
                    "compliance_counts": counts,
                }
    
        return results, labels, tie_decisions, audit, quality
    
    
    def dump(path, document):
        with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
            json.dump(document, handle, sort_keys=True, indent=1)
            handle.write("\n")
    
    
    def region_document(extract, results, region):
        events = []
        totals = {key: 0 for key in REGION_TOTAL_KEYS}
        for event_id in extract.event_ids:
            result = results[(event_id, region)]
            intervals = []
            for slot in range(extract.events[event_id]["interval_count"]):
                target = result["targets"][slot]
                relief = result["relief"][slot]
                intervals.append(
                    {
                        "interval": slot + 1,
                        "target_ticks": target,
                        "relief_ticks": relief,
                        "recovery_ticks": result["recovery"][slot],
                        "shortfall_ticks": max(0, target - relief),
                        "overdelivery_ticks": max(0, relief - target),
                    }
                )
            events.append(
                {
                    "event_id": event_id,
                    "candidate_offer_count": result["candidate_offer_count"],
                    "selected_offer_ids": result["selected_ids"],
                    "intervals": intervals,
                    "bid_cost_cents": result["bid_cost_cents"],
                    "credit_cents": result["credit_cents"],
                    "grant_pool_cents": result["grant_pool_cents"],
                    "grant_funded_cents": result["grant_funded_cents"],
                    "grant_allocated_cents": result["grant_allocated_cents"],
                    "grant_unallocated_cents": 
result["grant_unallocated_cents"],
                    "grants": [
                        {"beneficiary_id": beneficiary, "cents": cents}
                        for beneficiary, cents in 
sorted(result["grants"].items())
                    ],
                    "compliance_counts": result["compliance_counts"],
                }
            )
            totals["event_count"] += 1
            totals["candidate_offer_count"] += result["candidate_offer_count"]
            totals["selected_offer_count"] += len(result["selected_ids"])
            totals["relief_ticks"] += sum(result["relief"])
            totals["recovery_ticks"] += sum(result["recovery"])
            totals["shortfall_ticks"] += sum(item["shortfall_ticks"] for item in
intervals)
            totals["overdelivery_ticks"] += sum(
                item["overdelivery_ticks"] for item in intervals
            )
            for key in (
                "bid_cost_cents",
                "credit_cents",
                "grant_pool_cents",
                "grant_funded_cents",
                "grant_allocated_cents",
                "grant_unallocated_cents",
            ):
                totals[key] += result[key]
        return {"region": region, "events": events, "totals": totals}
    
    
    def segment_document(extract, results, segment):
        entries = []
        totals = {key: 0 for key in SEGMENT_TOTAL_KEYS}
        for event_id in extract.event_ids:
            for region in extract.region_names:
                result = results[(event_id, region)]
                offer_ids = []
                relief_ticks = 0
                recovery_ticks = 0
                bid_cost_cents = 0
                credit_cents = 0
                for offer_id in result["selected_ids"]:
                    offer = extract.offers[offer_id]
                    site = extract.sites[(offer["event_id"], offer["site_id"])]
                    if site["segment"] != segment:
                        continue
                    offer_ids.append(offer_id)
                    relief_ticks += offer["relief_total"]
                    recovery_ticks += offer["recovery_total"]
                    bid_cost_cents += offer["bid_cost_cents"]
                    credit_cents += result["offer_credits"][offer_id]
                floor_ticks = extract.floor_of(event_id, region, segment)[0]
                if floor_ticks == 0:
                    attainment = 10000
                else:
                    attainment = min(10000, half_up(10000 * relief_ticks, 
floor_ticks))
                entries.append(
                    {
                        "event_id": event_id,
                        "region": region,
                        "selected_offer_ids": sorted(offer_ids),
                        "relief_ticks": relief_ticks,
                        "recovery_ticks": recovery_ticks,
                        "bid_cost_cents": bid_cost_cents,
                        "credit_cents": credit_cents,
                        "floor_ticks": floor_ticks,
                        "floor_attainment_bp": attainment,
                    }
                )
                totals["selected_offer_count"] += len(offer_ids)
                totals["relief_ticks"] += relief_ticks
                totals["recovery_ticks"] += recovery_ticks
                totals["bid_cost_cents"] += bid_cost_cents
                totals["credit_cents"] += credit_cents
                totals["floor_ticks"] += floor_ticks
        return {"segment": segment, "event_regions": entries, "totals": totals}
    
    
    def write_outputs(extract, out_dir, results, labels, tie_decisions, audit, 
quality):
        os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)
    
        region_docs = {}
        for region in extract.region_names:
            region_docs[region] = region_document(extract, results, region)
            dump(os.path.join(out_dir, "region_%s.json" % region), 
region_docs[region])
    
        segment_docs = {}
        for segment in extract.segment_names:
            segment_docs[segment] = segment_document(extract, results, segment)
            dump(os.path.join(out_dir, "segment_%s.json" % segment), 
segment_docs[segment])
    
        summary = {
            "event_count": len(extract.event_ids),
            "event_region_count": len(extract.event_ids) * 
len(extract.region_names),
            "site_count": len(extract.sites),
        }
        for key in REGION_TOTAL_KEYS:
            if key == "event_count":
                continue
            summary[key] = sum(doc["totals"][key] for doc in 
region_docs.values())
        summary["regions"] = {
            region: {
                key: doc["totals"][key]
                for key in (
                    "selected_offer_count",
                    "relief_ticks",
                    "bid_cost_cents",
                    "credit_cents",
                    "grant_allocated_cents",
                )
            }
            for region, doc in region_docs.items()
        }
        summary["segments"] = {
            segment: {
                key: doc["totals"][key]
                for key in (
                    "selected_offer_count",
                    "relief_ticks",
                    "bid_cost_cents",
                    "credit_cents",
                )
            }
            for segment, doc in segment_docs.items()
        }
        dump(os.path.join(out_dir, "summary.json"), summary)
    
        counts = {label: 0 for label in LABELS}
        for label in labels.values():
            counts[label] += 1
        dump(
            os.path.join(out_dir, "policy_audit.json"),
            {
                "compliance_counts": counts,
                "site_labels": [
                    {"event_id": event_id, "site_id": site_id, "label": 
labels[key]}
                    for key in sorted(labels)
                    for event_id, site_id in (key,)
                ],
                "tie_decisions": tie_decisions,
                "cap_binding_count": audit["cap_binding_count"],
                "residual_cents": audit["residual_cents"],
                "residual_tie_pool_count": audit["residual_tie_pool_count"],
            },
        )
    
        document = dict(quality)
        document["rows"] = dict(extract.rows)
        document["orphan_offer_rows"] = extract.orphan_offer_rows
        document["orphan_profile_rows"] = extract.orphan_profile_rows
        document["out_of_range_profile_rows"] = 
extract.out_of_range_profile_rows
        document["duplicate_profile_rows"] = extract.duplicate_profile_rows
        document["unknown_region_site_rows"] = extract.unknown_region_site_rows
        document["waiver_signed_missing"] = extract.waiver_signed_missing
        document["zero_floor_count"] = sum(
            1
            for event_id in extract.event_ids
            for region in extract.region_names
            for segment in extract.segment_names
            if extract.floor_of(event_id, region, segment)[0] == 0
        )
        dump(os.path.join(out_dir, "data_quality.json"), document)
    
    
    def main(argv):
        if len(argv) != 3:
            sys.stderr.write("usage: derive_report.py DATA_DIR OUT_DIR\n")
            return 2
        extract = Extract(argv[1])
        results, labels, tie_decisions, audit, quality = derive(extract)
        write_outputs(extract, argv[2], results, labels, tie_decisions, audit, 
quality)
        return 0
    
    
    if __name__ == "__main__":
        sys.exit(main(sys.argv))

+50|def half_up(num, den):
+51|    return (2 * num + den) // (2 * den)
+52|
+53|
+54|def half_even(num, den):
+55|    quotient, rest = divmod(num, den)
+56|    twice = 2 * rest
+57|    if twice > den or (twice == den and quotient % 2):
+58|        quotient += 1
+59|    return quotient

+286|            entry = json.loads(line)
+287|            key = (entry["event_id"], entry["site_id"])
+288|            if key in self.sites:
+289|                self.state[key] = entry
+290|
+291|    # -------------------------------------------------------------- 
waivers
+292|    def load_waivers(self):
+293|        records = read_delimited(self.path("continuity_waivers.csv"), ",")
+294|        self.rows["continuity_waivers.csv"] = len(records)
+295|        self.waiver_signed_missing = 0
+296|        self.waivers = {}
+297|        for record in records:
+298|            if record["waiver_signed"].strip() == "":
+299|                self.waiver_signed_missing += 1
+300|            key = (record["event_id"], record["site_id"])
+301|            if key in self.sites:
+302|                self.waivers[key] = record
+303|
+304|    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- 
floors
+305|    def load_floors(self):
+306|        with open(self.path("segment_floors.json"), encoding="utf-8") as 
handle:
+307|            entries = json.load(handle)["floors"]
+308|        self.rows["segment_floors.json"] = len(entries)
+309|        self.floors = {}
+310|        for entry in entries:
+311|            key = (
+312|                entry["event_id"],
+313|                self.region_name(entry["region_code"]),
+314|                self.segment_name(entry["segment_code"]),
+315|            )
+316|            self.floors[key] = (
+317|                self.ticks(int(entry["floor_wh"])),
+318|                int(entry["priority_weight"]),
+319|            )
+320|
+321|    def floor_of(self, event_id, region, segment):
+322|        return self.floors.get((event_id, region, segment), (0, 0))
+323|
+324|    # ------------------------------------------------------------ rate 
cards
+325|    def load_rate_cards(self):
+326|        cards = 
ET.parse(self.path("rate_cards.xml")).getroot().findall("card")
+327|        self.rows["rate_cards.xml"] = len(cards)
+328|        self.cards = {}
+329|        for card in cards:
+330|            key = (card.get("event"), card.get("site"))
+331|            if key not in self.sites:
+332|                continue
+333|            override = card.find("override")
+334|            start = card.find("base") if override is None else override
+335|            self.cards[key] = (int(start.text), int(card.find("cap").text))
+336|
+337|    # --------------------------------------------------------------- 
weights
+338|    def load_weights(self):
+339|        records = read_delimited(self.path("recipient_weights.tsv"), "\t")
+340|        self.rows["recipient_weights.tsv"] = len(records)
+341|        self.grant_weights = {}
+342|        for record in records:
+343|            key = (record["event_id"], record["site_id"])
+344|            if key in self.sites:
+345|                self.grant_weights[key] = int(record["grant_weight"])
+346|
+347|    # ----------------------------------------------------------------- 
pools
+348|    def load_pools(self):
+349|        lines = read_record_lines(self.path("pools.txt"))
+350|        self.rows["pools.txt"] = len(lines)
+351|        self.pools = {}
+352|        for line in lines:
+353|            key = (line[0:10].strip(), self.region_name(line[10:14]))
+354|            self.pools[key] = int(line[14:26])
+355|
+356|    # ------------------------------------------------------------ 
allowances
+357|    def load_allowances(self):
+358|        records = 
read_commented_delimited(self.path("site_allowances.tsv"), "\t")
+359|        self.rows["site_allowances.tsv"] = len(records)
+360|        self.allowance_ticks = {}
+361|        draws_from = {}
+362|        for record in records:
+363|            site_id = record["site_id"]
+364|            self.allowance_ticks[site_id] = int(record["allowance_ticks"])
+365|            draws_from[site_id] = (record.get("draws_from") or "").strip()
+366|        self.allowance_root = {}
+367|        for site_id in self.allowance_ticks:
+368|            chain = [site_id]
+369|            current = site_id
+370|            while draws_from.get(current):
+371|                current = draws_from[current]
+372|                chain.append(current)
+373|            for node in chain:
+374|                self.allowance_root[node] = current
+375|
+376|    def root_of(self, site_id):
+377|        return self.allowance_root.get(site_id, site_id)
+378|
+379|
+380|def compliance_label(extract, key, site):
+381|    state = extract.state.get(key)
+382|    if state is not None:
+383|        if state["safety_hold"]:
+384|            return "SAFETY_HOLD"
+385|        if state["cooldown"]:
+386|            return "COOLDOWN"
+387|    waiver = extract.waivers.get(key)
+388|    if waiver is None or waiver["routine_certificate_pending"] != "Y":
+389|        return "CLEAR"
+390|    waiver_valid = (
+391|        waiver["waiver_status"] == "VALID"
+392|        and waiver["waiver_signed"] == "Y"
+393|        and site["critical"]
+394|        and site["segment"] == "public"
+395|    )
+396|    return "CONTINUITY_WAIVED" if waiver_valid else "CERTIFICATE_PENDING"
+397|
+398|
+399|def choose_portfolio(groups, targets, ceilings, floors, objective, 
interval_count):
+400|    """Pick the best feasible portfolio by section 5.
+401|
+402|    groups holds one list of (offer, segment index) pairs per site with
+403|    candidate offers. Returns (selected offer ids sorted, tied portfolio 
count).
+404|    """
+405|    target_ticks, shortfall_weight = targets
+406|    relief_ceiling, recovery_ceiling = ceilings
+407|    cost_weight, recovery_weight = objective
+408|    segment_count = len(floors)
+409|    relief = [0] * interval_count
+410|    recovery = [0] * interval_count
+411|    segment_relief = [0] * segment_count
+412|    chosen = []
+413|    best_key = None
+414|    best_ids = ()
+415|    tied = 0
+416|
+417|    def evaluate(bid_cost):
+418|        nonlocal best_key, best_ids, tied
+419|        weighted_shortfall = 0
+420|        overdelivery = 0
+421|        recovery_sum = 0
+422|        for index in range(interval_count):
+423|            gap = target_ticks[index] - relief[index]
+424|            if gap > 0:
+425|                weighted_shortfall += shortfall_weight[index] * gap
+426|            elif gap < 0:
+427|                overdelivery -= gap
+428|            recovery_sum += recovery[index]
+429|        floor_deficit = 0
+430|        for index in range(segment_count):
+431|            floor_ticks, priority_weight = floors[index]
+432|            deficit = floor_ticks - segment_relief[index]
+433|            if deficit > 0:
+434|                floor_deficit += priority_weight * deficit
+435|        key = (
+436|            weighted_shortfall,
+437|            floor_deficit,
+438|            cost_weight * bid_cost + recovery_weight * recovery_sum,
+439|            overdelivery,
+440|            len(chosen),
+441|        )
+442|        if best_key is None or key < best_key:
+443|            best_key = key
+444|            best_ids = tuple(sorted(chosen))
+445|            tied = 1
+446|        elif key == best_key:
+447|            tied += 1
+448|            ids = tuple(sorted(chosen))
+449|            if ids > best_ids:
+450|                best_ids = ids
+451|
+452|    def recurse(index, bid_cost):
+453|        if index == len(groups):
+454|            evaluate(bid_cost)
+455|            return
+456|        recurse(index + 1, bid_cost)
+457|        for offer, segment_index in groups[index]:
+458|            offer_relief = offer["relief"]
+459|            offer_recovery = offer["recovery"]
+460|            feasible = True
+461|            for slot in range(interval_count):
+462|                relief[slot] += offer_relief[slot]
+463|                recovery[slot] += offer_recovery[slot]
+464|                if (
+465|                    relief[slot] > relief_ceiling[slot]
+466|                    or recovery[slot] > recovery_ceiling[slot]
+467|                ):
+468|                    feasible = False
+469|            if feasible:
+470|                segment_relief[segment_index] += offer["relief_total"]
+471|                chosen.append(offer["offer_id"])
+472|                recurse(index + 1, bid_cost + offer["bid_cost_cents"])
+473|                chosen.pop()
+474|                segment_relief[segment_index] -= offer["relief_total"]
+475|            for slot in range(interval_count):
+476|                relief[slot] -= offer_relief[slot]
+477|                recovery[slot] -= offer_recovery[slot]
+478|
+479|    recurse(0, 0)
+480|    return list(best_ids), tied
+481|
+482|
+483|def largest_remainder(funded, weights):
+484|    """Share funded cents in proportion to weights; returns shares, 
leftover, tie."""
+485|    total = sum(weights.values())
+486|    shares = {}
+487|    remainders = {}
+488|    for beneficiary, weight in weights.items():
+489|        product = funded * weight
+490|        shares[beneficiary] = product // total
+491|        remainders[beneficiary] = product % total
+492|    leftover = funded - sum(shares.values())
+493|    order = sorted(weights, reverse=True)
+494|    order.sort(key=lambda beneficiary: -remainders[beneficiary])
+495|    for beneficiary in order[:leftover]:
+496|        shares[beneficiary] += 1
+497|    seen = defaultdict(int)
+498|    for remainder in remainders.values():
+499|        if remainder:
+500|            seen[remainder] += 1
+501|    return shares, leftover, any(count >= 2 for count in seen.values())
+502|
+503|
+504|def apportion(funded, weights, claim_per_weight, minimum):
+505|    """Section 7: returns shares, allocated, unallocated, residual, 
residual tie."""
+506|    positive = {
+507|        beneficiary: weight for beneficiary, weight in weights.items() if 
weight > 0
+508|    }
+509|    if not positive:
+510|        return {}, 0, funded, 0, False
+511|    claims = {
+512|        beneficiary: weight * claim_per_weight
+513|        for beneficiary, weight in positive.items()
+514|    }
+515|    if sum(claims.values()) <= funded:
+516|        allocated = sum(claims.values())
+517|        return claims, allocated, funded - allocated, 0, False
+518|    shares, residual, tie = largest_remainder(funded, positive)
+519|    below = [
+520|        beneficiary for beneficiary, cents in shares.items() if cents < 
minimum
+521|    ]
+522|    if len(below) == len(positive):
+523|        return {beneficiary: 0 for beneficiary in positive}, 0, funded, 0, 
False
+524|    if below:
+525|        keep = {
+526|            beneficiary: weight
+527|            for beneficiary, weight in positive.items()
+528|            if beneficiary not in below
+529|        }
+530|        shares, residual, tie = largest_remainder(funded, keep)
+531|        for beneficiary in below:
+532|            shares[beneficiary] = 0
+533|    allocated = sum(shares.values())
+534|    return shares, allocated, funded - allocated, residual, tie
+535|
+536|
+537|def derive(extract):
+538|    """Work the season through in season order; returns per event-region 
results."""
+539|    credit_denominator = 1000 * extract.millicents_per_cent
+540|
+541|    site_rows = defaultdict(list)
+542|    for key in sorted(extract.sites):
+543|        site = extract.sites[key]
+544|        site_rows[(site["event_id"], site["region"])].append(key)
+545|
+546|    labels = {key: compliance_label(extract, key, extract.sites[key]) for 
key in extract.sites}
+547|
+548|    effective_rate = {}
+549|    cap_binding = {}
+550|    for key, site in extract.sites.items():
+551|        card = extract.cards.get(key)
+552|        if card is None:
+553|            continue
+554|        rate, cap = card
+555|        rate += extract.segment_adder.get(site["segment"], 0)
+556|        rate += extract.events[site["event_id"]]["uplift"]
+557|        effective_rate[key] = min(rate, cap)
+558|        cap_binding[key] = rate > cap
+559|
+560|    offers_of_site_row = defaultdict(list)
+561|    for offer_id in sorted(extract.offers):
+562|        offer = extract.offers[offer_id]
+563|        offers_of_site_row[(offer["event_id"], 
offer["site_id"])].append(offer)
+564|
+565|    segment_index = {name: index for index, name in 
enumerate(extract.segment_names)}
+566|
+567|    audit = {
+568|        "cap_binding_count": 0,
+569|        "residual_cents": 0,
+570|        "residual_tie_pool_count": 0,
+571|    }
+572|    quality = {
+573|        "ineligible_offer_count": 0,
+574|        "allowance_blocked_offer_count": 0,
+575|        "empty_candidate_groups": 0,
+576|        "unallocated_pool_count": 0,
+577|    }
+578|    tie_decisions = []
+579|    drawn = defaultdict(int)
+580|    carried = defaultdict(int)
+581|    results = {}
+582|
+583|    for event_id in extract.season_order:
+584|        event = extract.events[event_id]
+585|        interval_count = event["interval_count"]
+586|
+587|        # Candidate offers of the whole event, before any of its own draws.
+588|        candidates = {region: defaultdict(list) for region in 
extract.region_names}
+589|        for region in extract.region_names:
+590|            for key in site_rows[(event_id, region)]:
+591|                site = extract.sites[key]
+592|                state = extract.state.get(key)
+593|                can_dispatch = state is not None and 
state["dispatch_authorized"]
+594|                root = extract.root_of(site["site_id"])
+595|                remaining = extract.allowance_ticks.get(root, 0) - 
drawn[root]
+596|                for offer in offers_of_site_row[key]:
+597|                    if not can_dispatch:
+598|                        quality["ineligible_offer_count"] += 1
+599|                    elif offer["relief_total"] > remaining:
+600|                        quality["allowance_blocked_offer_count"] += 1
+601|                    else:
+602|                        candidates[region][site["site_id"]].append(offer)
+603|
+604|        for region in extract.region_names:
+605|            groups = []
+606|            candidate_offer_count = 0
+607|            for site_id in sorted(candidates[region]):
+608|                offers = candidates[region][site_id]
+609|                segment = extract.sites[(event_id, site_id)]["segment"]
+610|                groups.append([(offer, segment_index[segment]) for offer in
offers])
+611|                candidate_offer_count += len(offers)
+612|            if candidate_offer_count == 0:
+613|                quality["empty_candidate_groups"] += 1
+614|
+615|            targets = extract.target_of(event_id, region)
+616|            floors = [
+617|                extract.floor_of(event_id, region, segment)
+618|                for segment in extract.segment_names
+619|            ]
+620|            selected_ids, tied = choose_portfolio(
+621|                groups,
+622|                targets,
+623|                extract.ceiling_of(event_id, region),
+624|                floors,
+625|                (event["cost_weight"], event["recovery_weight"]),
+626|                interval_count,
+627|            )
+628|            if tied >= 2:
+629|                tie_decisions.append(
+630|                    {
+631|                        "event_id": event_id,
+632|                        "region": region,
+633|                        "tied_portfolio_count": tied,
+634|                        "selected_offer_ids": list(selected_ids),
+635|                    }
+636|                )
+637|
+638|            relief = [0] * interval_count
+639|            recovery = [0] * interval_count
+640|            bid_cost_cents = 0
+641|            credit_cents = 0
+642|            offer_credits = {}
+643|            for offer_id in selected_ids:
+644|                offer = extract.offers[offer_id]
+645|                key = (offer["event_id"], offer["site_id"])
+646|                bid_cost_cents += offer["bid_cost_cents"]
+647|                rate = effective_rate.get(key, 0)
+648|                credit = 0
+649|                for slot in range(interval_count):
+650|                    relief[slot] += offer["relief"][slot]
+651|                    recovery[slot] += offer["recovery"][slot]
+652|                    credited = offer["relief"][slot] - 
offer["recovery"][slot]
+653|                    if credited > 0:
+654|                        credit += half_even(
+655|                            credited * extract.wh_per_tick * rate, 
credit_denominator
+656|                        )
+657|                offer_credits[offer_id] = credit
+658|                credit_cents += credit
+659|                if cap_binding.get(key):
+660|                    audit["cap_binding_count"] += 1
+661|                drawn[extract.root_of(offer["site_id"])] += 
offer["relief_total"]
+662|
+663|            pool_cents = extract.pools.get((event_id, region), 0)
+664|            funded_cents = max(0, pool_cents + carried[region] - 
credit_cents)
+665|            weights = defaultdict(int)
+666|            recipients = 0
+667|            for key in site_rows[(event_id, region)]:
+668|                if key not in extract.grant_weights:
+669|                    continue
+670|                recipients += 1
+671|                weight = extract.grant_weights[key]
+672|                if labels[key] == "CONTINUITY_WAIVED":
+673|                    weight *= 2
+674|                weights[extract.sites[key]["beneficiary_id"]] += weight
+675|            shares, allocated, unallocated, residual, tie = apportion(
+676|                funded_cents,
+677|                dict(weights),
+678|                extract.claim_cents_per_weight,
+679|                extract.minimum_grant_cents,
+680|            )
+681|            if recipients == 0 or sum(weights.values()) == 0:
+682|                quality["unallocated_pool_count"] += 1
+683|            audit["residual_cents"] += residual
+684|            if tie:
+685|                audit["residual_tie_pool_count"] += 1
+686|            carried[region] = unallocated
+687|
+688|            counts = {label: 0 for label in LABELS}
+689|            for key in site_rows[(event_id, region)]:
+690|                counts[labels[key]] += 1
+691|
+692|            results[(event_id, region)] = {
+693|                "candidate_offer_count": candidate_offer_count,
+694|                "selected_ids": list(selected_ids),
+695|                "offer_credits": offer_credits,
+696|                "relief": relief,
+697|                "recovery": recovery,
+698|                "targets": targets[0],
+699|                "bid_cost_cents": bid_cost_cents,
+700|                "credit_cents": credit_cents,
+701|                "grant_pool_cents": pool_cents,
+702|                "grant_funded_cents": funded_cents,
+703|                "grant_allocated_cents": allocated,
+704|                "grant_unallocated_cents": unallocated,
+705|                "grants": shares,
+706|                "compliance_counts": counts,
+707|            }
+708|
+709|    return results, labels, tie_decisions, audit, quality
+710|
+711|
+712|def dump(path, document):
+713|    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
+714|        json.dump(document, handle, sort_keys=True, indent=1)
+715|        handle.write("\n")
+716|
+717|
+718|def region_document(extract, results, region):
+719|    events = []
+720|    totals = {key: 0 for key in REGION_TOTAL_KEYS}
+721|    for event_id in extract.event_ids:
+722|        result = results[(event_id, region)]
+723|        intervals = []
+724|        for slot in range(extract.events[event_id]["interval_count"]):
+725|            target = result["targets"][slot]
+726|            relief = result["relief"][slot]
+727|            intervals.append(
+728|                {
+729|                    "interval": slot + 1,
+730|                    "target_ticks": target,
+731|                    "relief_ticks": relief,
+732|                    "recovery_ticks": result["recovery"][slot],
+733|                    "shortfall_ticks": max(0, target - relief),
+734|                    "overdelivery_ticks": max(0, relief - target),
+735|                }
+736|            )
+737|        events.append(
+738|            {
+739|                "event_id": event_id,
+740|                "candidate_offer_count": result["candidate_offer_count"],
+741|                "selected_offer_ids": result["selected_ids"],
+742|                "intervals": intervals,
+743|                "bid_cost_cents": result["bid_cost_cents"],
+744|                "credit_cents": result["credit_cents"],
+745|                "grant_pool_cents": result["grant_pool_cents"],
+746|                "grant_funded_cents": result["grant_funded_cents"],
+747|                "grant_allocated_cents": result["grant_allocated_cents"],
+748|                "grant_unallocated_cents": 
result["grant_unallocated_cents"],
+749|                "grants": [
+750|                    {"beneficiary_id": beneficiary, "cents": cents}
+751|                    for beneficiary, cents in 
sorted(result["grants"].items())
+752|                ],
+753|                "compliance_counts": result["compliance_counts"],
+754|            }
+755|        )
+756|        totals["event_count"] += 1
+757|        totals["candidate_offer_count"] += result["candidate_offer_count"]
+758|        totals["selected_offer_count"] += len(result["selected_ids"])
+759|        totals["relief_ticks"] += sum(result["relief"])
+760|        totals["recovery_ticks"] += sum(result["recovery"])
+761|        totals["shortfall_ticks"] += sum(item["shortfall_ticks"] for item 
in intervals)
+762|        totals["overdelivery_ticks"] += sum(
+763|            item["overdelivery_ticks"] for item in intervals
+764|        )
+765|        for key in (
+766|            "bid_cost_cents",
+767|            "credit_cents",
+768|            "grant_pool_cents",
+769|            "grant_funded_cents",
+770|            "grant_allocated_cents",
+771|            "grant_unallocated_cents",
+772|        ):
+773|            totals[key] += result[key]
+774|    return {"region": region, "events": events, "totals": totals}
+775|
+776|
+777|def segment_document(extract, results, segment):
+778|    entries = []
+779|    totals = {key: 0 for key in SEGMENT_TOTAL_KEYS}
+780|    for event_id in extract.event_ids:
+781|        for region in extract.region_names:
+782|            result = results[(event_id, region)]
+783|            offer_ids = []
+784|            relief_ticks = 0
+785|            recovery_ticks = 0
+786|            bid_cost_cents = 0
+787|            credit_cents = 0
+788|            for offer_id in result["selected_ids"]:
+789|                offer = extract.offers[offer_id]
+790|                site = extract.sites[(offer["event_id"], offer["site_id"])]
+791|                if site["segment"] != segment:
+792|                    continue
+793|                offer_ids.append(offer_id)
+794|                relief_ticks += offer["relief_total"]
+795|                recovery_ticks += offer["recovery_total"]
+796|                bid_cost_cents += offer["bid_cost_cents"]
+797|                credit_cents += result["offer_credits"][offer_id]
+798|            floor_ticks = extract.floor_of(event_id, region, segment)[0]
+799|            if floor_ticks == 0:
+800|                attainment = 10000
+801|            else:
+802|                attainment = min(10000, half_up(10000 * relief_ticks, 
floor_ticks))
+803|            entries.append(
+804|                {
+805|                    "event_id": event_id,
+806|                    "region": region,
+807|                    "selected_offer_ids": sorted(offer_ids),
+808|                    "relief_ticks": relief_ticks,
+809|                    "recovery_ticks": recovery_ticks,
+810|                    "bid_cost_cents": bid_cost_cents,
+811|                    "credit_cents": credit_cents,
+812|                    "floor_ticks": floor_ticks,
+813|                    "floor_attainment_bp": attainment,
+814|                }
+815|            )
+816|            totals["selected_offer_count"] += len(offer_ids)
+817|            totals["relief_ticks"] += relief_ticks
+818|            totals["recovery_ticks"] += recovery_ticks
+819|            totals["bid_cost_cents"] += bid_cost_cents
+820|            totals["credit_cents"] += credit_cents
+821|            totals["floor_ticks"] += floor_ticks
+822|    return {"segment": segment, "event_regions": entries, "totals": totals}
+823|
+824|
+825|def write_outputs(extract, out_dir, results, labels, tie_decisions, audit, 
quality):
+826|    os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)
+827|
+828|    region_docs = {}
+829|    for region in extract.region_names:
+830|        region_docs[region] = region_document(extract, results, region)
+831|        dump(os.path.join(out_dir, "region_%s.json" % region), 
region_docs[region])
+832|
+833|    segment_docs = {}
+834|    for segment in extract.segment_names:
+835|        segment_docs[segment] = segment_document(extract, results, segment)
+836|        dump(os.path.join(out_dir, "segment_%s.json" % segment), 
segment_docs[segment])
+837|
+838|    summary = {
+839|        "event_count": len(extract.event_ids),
+840|        "event_region_count": len(extract.event_ids) * 
len(extract.region_names),
+841|        "site_count": len(extract.sites),
+842|    }
+843|    for key in REGION_TOTAL_KEYS:
+844|        if key == "event_count":
+845|            continue
+846|        summary[key] = sum(doc["totals"][key] for doc in 
region_docs.values())
+847|    summary["regions"] = {
+848|        region: {
+849|            key: doc["totals"][key]
+850|            for key in (
+851|                "selected_offer_count",
+852|                "relief_ticks",
+853|                "bid_cost_cents",
+854|                "credit_cents",
+855|                "grant_allocated_cents",
+856|            )
+857|        }
+858|        for region, doc in region_docs.items()
+859|    }
+860|    summary["segments"] = {
+861|        segment: {
+862|            key: doc["totals"][key]
+863|            for key in (
+864|                "selected_offer_count",
+865|                "relief_ticks",
+866|                "bid_cost_cents",
+867|                "credit_cents",
+868|            )
+869|        }
+870|        for segment, doc in segment_docs.items()
+871|    }
+872|    dump(os.path.join(out_dir, "summary.json"), summary)
+873|
+874|    counts = {label: 0 for label in LABELS}
+875|    for label in labels.values():
+876|        counts[label] += 1
+877|    dump(
+878|        os.path.join(out_dir, "policy_audit.json"),
+879|        {
+880|            "compliance_counts": counts,
+881|            "site_labels": [
+882|                {"event_id": event_id, "site_id": site_id, "label": 
labels[key]}
+883|                for key in sorted(labels)
+884|                for event_id, site_id in (key,)
+885|            ],
+886|            "tie_decisions": tie_decisions,
+887|            "cap_binding_count": audit["cap_binding_count"],
+888|            "residual_cents": audit["residual_cents"],
+889|            "residual_tie_pool_count": audit["residual_tie_pool_count"],
+890|        },
+891|    )
+892|
+893|    document = dict(quality)
+894|    document["rows"] = dict(extract.rows)
+895|    document["orphan_offer_rows"] = extract.orphan_offer_rows
+896|    document["orphan_profile_rows"] = extract.orphan_profile_rows
+897|    document["out_of_range_profile_rows"] = 
extract.out_of_range_profile_rows
+898|    document["duplicate_profile_rows"] = extract.duplicate_profile_rows
+899|    document["unknown_region_site_rows"] = extract.unknown_region_site_rows
+900|    document["waiver_signed_missing"] = extract.waiver_signed_missing
+901|    document["zero_floor_count"] = sum(
+902|        1
+903|        for event_id in extract.event_ids
+904|        for region in extract.region_names
+905|        for segment in extract.segment_names
+906|        if extract.floor_of(event_id, region, segment)[0] == 0
+907|    )
+908|    dump(os.path.join(out_dir, "data_quality.json"), document)
+909|
+910|
+911|def main(argv):
+912|    if len(argv) != 3:
+913|        sys.stderr.write("usage: derive_report.py DATA_DIR OUT_DIR\n")
+914|        return 2
+915|    extract = Extract(argv[1])
+916|    results, labels, tie_decisions, audit, quality = derive(extract)
+917|    write_outputs(extract, argv[2], results, labels, tie_decisions, audit, 
quality)
+918|    return 0

import sys; sys.path.insert(0,'out')
import derive_report as dr, itertools, collections
ex=dr.Extract('data')
# independent brute-force check for a sample of event-regions
results,labels,ties,audit,quality=dr.derive(ex)
# rebuild candidates the same way but verify with itertools.product
import random
# recompute candidate sets by replaying season order
site_rows=collections.defaultdict(list)
for k,s in ex.sites.items(): site_rows[(s['event_id'],s['region'])].append(k)
offers_of=collections.defaultdict(list)
for oid,o in ex.offers.items(): 
offers_of[(o['event_id'],o['site_id'])].append(o)
drawn=collections.defaultdict(int)
mismatch=0; checked=0; tiecount=0
keyhist=collections.Counter()
for eid in ex.season_order:
    ev=ex.events[eid]; n=ev['interval_count']
    cand={r:collections.defaultdict(list) for r in ex.region_names}
    for r in ex.region_names:
        for k in site_rows[(eid,r)]:
            s=ex.sites[k]; st=ex.state.get(k)
            if not (st and st['dispatch_authorized']): continue
            root=ex.root_of(s['site_id']); 
rem=ex.allowance_ticks.get(root,0)-drawn[root]
            for o in sorted(offers_of[k],key=lambda o:o['offer_id']):
                if o['relief_total']<=rem: cand[r][s['site_id']].append(o)
    for r in ex.region_names:
        tt,sw=ex.target_of(eid,r); rc,vc=ex.ceiling_of(eid,r)
        fl=[ex.floor_of(eid,r,sg) for sg in ex.segment_names]
        segi={nm:i for i,nm in enumerate(ex.segment_names)}
        groups=[[None]+cand[r][sid] for sid in sorted(cand[r])]
        best=None; bestids=None; cnt=0
        for combo in itertools.product(*groups):
            sel=[o for o in combo if o]
            rel=[0]*n; rec=[0]*n; sr=[0]*len(fl); bid=0
            for o in sel:
                for t in range(n): rel[t]+=o['relief'][t]; 
rec[t]+=o['recovery'][t]
                sr[segi[ex.sites[(eid,o['site_id'])]['segment']]]+=o['relief_tot
al']; bid+=o['bid_cost_cents']
            if any(rel[t]>rc[t] or rec[t]>vc[t] for t in range(n)): continue
            c1=sum(sw[t]*max(0,tt[t]-rel[t]) for t in range(n))
            c2=sum(fl[i][1]*max(0,fl[i][0]-sr[i]) for i in range(len(fl)))
            c3=ev['cost_weight']*bid+ev['recovery_weight']*sum(rec)
            c4=sum(max(0,rel[t]-tt[t]) for t in range(n))
            key=(c1,c2,c3,c4,len(sel))
            ids=tuple(sorted(o['offer_id'] for o in sel))
            if best is None or key<best: best=key; bestids=ids; cnt=1
            elif key==best:
                cnt+=1
                if ids>bestids: bestids=ids
        checked+=1
        if cnt>1: tiecount+=1
        got=results[(eid,r)]['selected_ids']
        if list(bestids)!=got: mismatch+=1; print('MISMATCH',eid,r,bestids,got)
        for o in got: 
drawn[ex.root_of(ex.offers[o]['site_id'])]+=ex.offers[o]['relief_total']
print('checked',checked,'mismatch',mismatch,'tied groups',tiecount)

Reasoning:
Everything checks out against my manual calculations: claims paid, allocation 
with leftover going to the greatest id, mixed cases, below-minimum cases, and 
zero-weight cases all match expected results.

Now I'm designing a synthetic end-to-end test setup, building out a program 
config with unit conversions, region and segment codes, grant rate rules, and 
export settings, plus a set of events with carefully chosen timestamps 
(including a timezone offset case) to test ordering logic, and site records 
linking each event to regions and segments for verification.

I'm also constructing the sites table so it exercises code-to-region mapping 
across different codes resolving to the same region, includes an unknown region 
case that should be skipped, and connects benefit groups appropriately. Then I'm
setting up tied offers from two sites with identical bids and relief profiles to
test tie-breaking against the feeder ceiling.

Since recovery ceiling is 0, recovery must stay at 0, and with cost/recovery 
weights zeroed out, the decision reduces to picking the higher-id offer among 
the tied portfolios — so OA2 wins over OA1, and I'm logging this as a 
tie_decisions entry with tied_portfolio_count 2. Now I'm moving to the EV-A 
South case with site3's offer OA3, which has a higher bid and a more complex 
relief/recovery schedule across intervals.

For South, comparing the empty portfolio against including OA3: empty gives 
shortfall 20 under interval weighting, while OA3 fully covers the floor 
requirement with zero deficit, so OA3 is clearly selected. Then for crediting, 
since SITE3 is public, I calculate the rate as base 2000 plus a 1000 public 
adder, which exceeds the 2500 cap, so the cap binds and the effective credited 
rate becomes 2500.

Working through the ticks: interval1 gives 15 ticks and interval2 gives 0, but 
amount = round_half_even(15*10*2500/1e6) comes out to 0.375 which rounds down to
0 -- too small to test rounding behavior meaningfully. I'm scaling up the relief
to 2000 Wh (200 ticks) with recovery at 500 Wh (50 ticks), giving 150 credited 
ticks, which produces 3.75 -- but that rounds to 4 under both half-up and 
half-even, so I still need to find a case landing exactly on x.5 to actually 
distinguish the rounding modes.

I'm solving for credited*rate = 50000*(2k+1), and with rate 2500 that means 
credited=20 gives exactly 0.5, which rounds to 0 under half-even but 1 under 
half-up -- a clean discriminator. So I'm setting interval1 to relief 300 Wh / 
recovery 100 Wh (credited 20 ticks → 0.5 → rounds to 0), and interval2 to relief
500 Wh / recovery 0 (credited 50 ticks → 1.25 → rounds to 1), giving a total 
credit of 1. Now I need to adjust the targets accordingly.

Now I'm setting relief ceilings to 1000 Wh for both intervals, with the South 
EV-A targets at 100 Wh (weight 2) and 0 Wh (weight 1), and the South public 
floor at 150 Wh. Checking offer OA3 against the alternative: shortfall comes out
to 0 with overdelivery of 70, versus the empty case with shortfall 20 and floor 
deficit 15 -- since shortfall is evaluated first, OA3 wins as expected. Moving 
on to EV-B's offer OB1 at SITE1 with a 1000 Wh relief interval.

For SITE3's public offer OB3 in the South, I note it has zero relief since there
are no profile rows, confirming that intervals with no kept row default to zero.
I'm setting EV-B's cost and recovery weights to 1 each with a 1000 uplift, 
targeting 500 Wh in interval 1 for the North with a 2000 Wh ceiling -- comparing
the empty case (shortfall 50) against choosing OB1 (shortfall 0, overdelivery

adder, a 1000 uplift, and a card base of 3000 capped at 9000, the rate comes to 
4000 (not cap-binding), giving a credited amount of roughly 4 for the 100-tick 
interval.

Now I'm checking EV-B South, where OB3 relief is zero everywhere and the target 
is zero in both intervals, so the empty portfolio (cost 0) beats selecting OB3

I realize if SITE1's allowance is exactly 100, drawing OB1 fully at EV-B leaves 
zero remaining for EV-A, which blocks OA1 entirely and ruins my intended tie 
test — so I bump SITE1's allowance to 120, leaving 20 remaining at EV-A, just

Now I'm also filling in the remaining test rows: SITE4 needs a blank entry since

the groups, and the recovery ceiling of 0 for EV-A North is satisfied by both 
offers.

Now I'm moving into writing out the actual output files, starting with the 
feeder limits file in fixed-width format.

Writing fixed-width columns...

Checking B-OFFER-1 (100 ticks), B-OFFER-3 (no rows, all zero), A-OFFER-4 orphan 
at SITE4 (kept since offer_id exists), and NOPE-OFFER (orphan profile row). 
Tallying totals: 14 profile rows, with orphan_profile_rows=1, out_of_range=1, 
duplicate=1.

Now going through the seven rate cards, verifying caps and overrides apply 
correctly, especially SITE3's override case where the binding cap of 2500 kicks 
in over the raw 3000 rate, and confirming SITE4's unknown region card gets 
skipped entirely.

Checking each OA/OB credit calculation — multiplying credited ticks by rate and 
dividing by a million, then applying half-even rounding where results land 
exactly on .5, like OA3's interval 1 rounding down to 0 and interval 2 rounding 
up to 1, giving final credits of 8, 1, and 4 respectively. Now I'm moving on to 
verify the target definitions for region EV-A.

Filling out the remaining feeder limit rows to reach 12 total, then adding 
segment floor entries for EV-A N1 and EV-A S1, and starting on pool values for 
each region across both EVs.

Now I'm checking allowances and draws: SITE1 has 300 with no fallback, SITE2 
draws 0 from SITE1, and SITE3-5 have their own limits. Walking through the draw 
logic for EV-B and EV-A against SITE1's pool: EV-B's OB1 relief of 100 fits 
within the 300 remaining, leaving 200; then EV-A's OA2 option at 200 exactly 
fits while OA2b's 300 request gets blocked correctly, confirming OA2 is the 
valid choice drawing 300 total. Moving on to check SITE3's remaining capacity 
against EV-B's request.

For EV-B South, OB3's relief of 0 and selecting the empty set wins over choosing
{OB3} based on the tie-break ordering (0,0,100,0,1) versus (0,0,0,0,0), 
confirming the selected set should be empty. With this validated, I'm ready to 
write the synthetic extract and assertion script, placing it in /tmp so it stays
outside the repo.

    def write(name, text):
        with open(os.path.join(D, name), "w") as fh:
            fh.write(text)
    
    
    write("program.ini", """# synthetic
    [units]
    wh_per_tick = 10
    millicents_per_cent = 1000

    def no_floats(node, path=""):
        if isinstance(node, bool):
            failures.append("boolean at %s" % path)
        elif isinstance(node, float):
            failures.append("float at %s" % path)
        elif isinstance(node, dict):
            for key, value in node.items():
                no_floats(value, path + "/" + str(key))
        elif isinstance(node, list):
            for index, value in enumerate(node):
                no_floats(value, "%s/%d" % (path, index))
    
    
    for name in sorted(os.listdir(O)):
        no_floats(load(name), name)
    
    if failures:
        print("FAILURES")
        for item in failures:
            print(" ", item)
        sys.exit(1)
    print("synthetic extract: all checks pass")

+361|def no_floats(node, path=""):
+362|    if isinstance(node, bool):
+363|        failures.append("boolean at %s" % path)
+364|    elif isinstance(node, float):
+365|        failures.append("float at %s" % path)
+366|    elif isinstance(node, dict):
+367|        for key, value in node.items():
+368|            no_floats(value, path + "/" + str(key))
+369|    elif isinstance(node, list):
+370|        for index, value in enumerate(node):
+371|            no_floats(value, "%s/%d" % (path, index))
+372|

+374|for name in sorted(os.listdir(O)):
+375|    no_floats(load(name), name)

even when a site has multiple offers in one event. For tie-breaking on season
